# Lantern Grove: Consolidated Prop List (for 3D Model Bot)

All sizes are approximate bounding boxes [x, y, z] in studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).
Every prop is decoration only: it has **no collision**, because collision comes from `holes.json`.
Exception: **moving parts must be separate meshes** with their pivot at the stated point, because RobloxDevBot animates them
and attaches the invisible collision blockers to them.

Priority: **P1** = needed for a playable, readable course. **P2** = strong theming. **P3** = polish.

## Moving meshes (separate parts, pivot required)

holes.json is the source of truth for all pivots and motion; this table mirrors it (updated 2026-10-10 for playtest Round 7;
**bold** = changed or new in Round 5, ⏱ = changed in Round 7: timing obstacles retuned with pauses, see playtest-log.md Round 7).
Sliders with pauses must follow holes.json `dwell` exactly (tween each leg at the listed speed, then wait); `period_s` is the full cycle.

| Prop | Hole | Size (studs) | Pivot / axis | Motion | Pri |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Puffball carousel bar (2 **padded** arms + toadstool hub cap) | H4 | **6 x 4 x 6 (arms 2.3 long from r 0.7 to 3.0, 0.7 thick, 1 high)** | Pivot at hub centre (0,0,-21 local), axis +Y | Rotates **30 deg/s** continuous. Arms are soft puffball bumpers (restitution 0.65) | P1 |
| Mill wheel (2 opposed paddles, rim, axle) | H6 | 1 x 7.2 x 8 (radius 3.6, paddles 7.6 wide) | Axle centre (7.5,3.6,-8.5 local), axis +Z | Rotates 30 deg/s continuous | P1 |
| ⏱ Sliding root door | H8 | 0.7 x 1 x **3.6** (was 3.2) | Centre (4.75,0,-11.5 local) = **closed** position, slides along +Z (south) | **Gate cycle 6.67 s: closed at offset 0 for 1.8 s, slides to +4.0 at 3 studs/s (1.33 s), open (hidden in the south root-knot pocket, door spans z -9.3..-5.7) for 2.2 s, slides back (1.33 s).** Was ping-pong -3.5..+3.5 at 2 studs/s, 7 s. Only the south pocket is needed now (door passes through the junction rail line z -8 by up to 2.3) | P1 |
| ⏱ Floating log on hidden rail | H7 | **6 x 0.8 x 0.8** (was 5) **with pointed ends**: the last 0.4 at each end tapers to a vertical 90° edge (whittled point / root knot). Collision: 5.2 x 0.8 x 0.8 box + a 0.566 x 0.8 x 0.566 box turned 45° at each end | Centre (0,-1.2,-20.5 local), slides along +X | **Ping-pong -6..+6 at 3.5 studs/s, pausing 1.0 s at the west end (-6) and 1.5 s at the east end (+6); cycle 9.36 s.** Log ends stop **1.0 stud** short of each rail line (x = ±10). Was ±7 at 5 studs/s, 0.5 short, no pauses, flat ends | P1 |
| Root portcullis gate | H9 | 0.8 x 1.4 x 7.4 | Base centre (6,1,-25.5 local), slides along +Y | Ping-pong **0..1.8 studs at 0.9 studs/s** (same 4 s cycle, open ~70%) | P1 |
| ⏱ Great lantern pendulum | H9 | Bob (square lantern body) **1.6 x 1.8 x 1.6**, **turned 30° about the vertical** (yaw 30 in holes.json), so it is 2.19 wide across the swing; chain 7 long from the hinge to the bob centre. **Collider foot 1.0 x 0.4 x 1.6 (x along the swing, z across), yaw 0 = square to the swing plane** (as built in Studio v131; Round 9 spec was 0.9, Round 8 0.8 x 0.4 x 1.6 at yaw 30). Centred on the bob axis 7.759 below the hinge; its bottom is 0.037 above the felt at rest (0.059 below the lantern's visible bottom) and ≥ 0.021 over the whole swing. **3D Model Bot: base plinth on the lantern bob mesh, 1.0 x 0.4 x 1.6** (no change in Round 10 if it was already made 1.0; if it was made to the Round 9 0.9 spec, lengthen it to 1.0 along the swing): yaw 0 in the course = **turned -30° relative to the lantern body**, centred under the body, bottom 0.059 below the body's bottom (top 0.341 up inside the body). Its corners stick out up to 0.14 past the body's faces. Without the plinth, an invisible collider would stop balls outside the visible lantern. Alternative: widen the lantern's bottom 0.4 to a 1.88 x 1.88 square (same yaw 30) reaching down 0.059 lower. The foot itself is a Transparency-1 CanCollide part (GolfMover group) welded to the bob | **Hinge (21.2, 8.996, -38.5 local)** (Round 8: x was 22.0, now right over the chamber shot line), axis +Z (swings east-west in the local x-y plane). Model the pivot at the hinge; bob centre 7.0 below it | **Swing: angle(t) = 11° x sin(360°·t / 4 s)** (Round 10: was 13°; Round 8: 30°, 5 s), peak 17.3 deg/s at the bottom. Lantern bottom hangs 0.096 above the felt at rest and the foot 0.037 (both ≥ 0.02 under their lowest corners mid-swing). In-engine a bob that meets only the top half of the ball lets it roll under (stage-h test), so the foot is what stops the ball. At the ends of the swing the lantern hangs beside the shot line (centre 1.34 studs east/west of the hinge; its near edge still clears the ball by ≥ 0.15). Sweep (lantern + foot) x 18.62..23.78, y 1.02..3.22, z -39.59..-37.41 | P1 |

# AGENTS.md: RobloxDev

Instructions for any coding agent (Codex, Cursor, Claude Code, Grok Bot) working in this repo. `CLAUDE.md` is a symlink to this file.

## What this repo is
- Shared dev work for Roblox games: **agent skills, per-game design notes, Blender scripts, exported assets.**
- **It is NOT game code.** Game scripts and instances live in Roblox Studio and are saved and published from Studio (Studio versioning, no Rojo or git sync). Don't try to mirror Studio scripts into this repo unless asked.
- We're vibe coding: optimize for a playable, published game. Don't do line-by-line review.

## Dev machine
Chase's Mac Mini (`chases-Mac-mini.local`, user `chase-mini`, Apple Silicon) runs:
- Roblox Studio at `/Applications/RobloxStudio.app`, with its built-in MCP server `/Applications/RobloxStudio.app/Contents/MacOS/StudioMCP`
- Blender 5.2 LTS at `/Applications/Blender.app` (`/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender`)
- Cursor (`cursor`, `cursor-agent`), Codex (`codex`, already configured with the Roblox Studio MCP), plus `gh`, `uv`/`uvx`, `node`, and `python3` (3.9; use `uv` for newer versions)
- This repo is cloned at `~/src/RobloxDev`

## Skills (`.agents/skills/`)
| Skill | Use when |
|---|---|
| `roblox-dev-workflow` | Any Roblox task: the overall operating model, which tool to use, and where things live |
| `roblox-studio-mcp` | Reading or changing anything in an open Studio place (scripts, instances, playtests) |
| `blender-asset-pipeline` | Making meshes or textures in Blender for Roblox |
| `roblox-publishing` | Taking a place from Studio to a live, public experience |
| `new-roblox-game` | Starting a new game or prototype |

Skills live in `.agents/skills/`, which Codex and Cursor read directly. `.claude/skills` is a symlink to it for Claude Code. Edit skills only in `.agents/skills/`.

## Layout
```
.agents/skills/<skill>/SKILL.md   canonical skills
.claude/skills -> ../.agents/skills
games/<game-name>/design.md       pitch, core loop, controls, MVP scope
games/<game-name>/status.md       current state, TODO, known bugs, place/universe IDs
assets/<game-name>/               Blender generator scripts (*.py) + exports (*.fbx, textures)
```

## Conventions
- Use kebab-case for game folder names. Record the Roblox place ID and universe ID in `games/<game>/status.md` once published.
- Commit notes, skills and asset scripts often, with short clear messages. Never commit secrets or API keys.
- When a workflow gets repeated or a lesson is learned, update the matching skill.

name: roblox-dev-workflow
description: Operating model for building Roblox games on Chase's Mac Mini. Covers which tool to use (Roblox Studio MCP first, computer use as a fallback), where code, notes and assets live, model choice, and the goal of shipping complete published games. Use at the start of any Roblox game-dev task or when unsure how to approach one.

# Roblox dev workflow

## Goal
Ship complete, playable games that are **published on Roblox**. Prioritize a working game loop over polish. We vibe code: no line-by-line review.

## Machine and tools (Mac Mini, `chases-Mac-mini.local`)
- **Roblox Studio** at `/Applications/RobloxStudio.app`. Its MCP server (`StudioMCP`) is the main way to work in a place. See the `roblox-studio-mcp` skill.
- **Blender 5.2** at `/Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender`, for meshes and textures. See `blender-asset-pipeline`.
- **Codex** (`codex`, including `codex exec` for runs nobody is watching) and **Cursor** (`cursor`, `cursor-agent`). Both already have the `Roblox_Studio` MCP configured.
- **Grok Bot** reaches the Mac Mini over remote shell. It drives Studio through `codex exec` (see `roblox-studio-mcp`) and Blender through headless scripts.

## Order of preference for doing things in Studio
1. **Roblox Studio MCP**: use it for scripts, instances, terrain and lighting via Luau, inserting assets, playtesting, reading console output and screenshots.
2. **Computer use** (Codex computer use, Cursor, or Grok Bot's desktop) **only** for things the MCP can't do: Studio UI clicks (File > Save/Publish, Game Settings dialogs, plugin or MCP toggles), the 3D Importer dialog, and the create.roblox.com dashboard.
3. Keep work reproducible: save Luau snippets or asset generator scripts in this repo when they're worth reusing.

## Where things live
- **Game code**: in Studio (the place file / cloud place), saved and published from Studio. Studio's version history is the source of truth. Don't use Rojo or git sync for now.
- **This repo (`~/src/RobloxDev`)**: skills (`.agents/skills/`), per-game notes (`games/<game-name>/design.md`, `status.md`), and asset scripts and exports (`assets/<game-name>/`).
- Update `games/<game>/status.md` at the end of each work session: what changed, what's next, known bugs, place and universe IDs.

## Models and delegation
- Use the strongest available model (e.g. Opus 5.5 or GPT-6 Astra) for hard 3D and spatial work: level layout, CFrame and rotation math, physics, camera, procedural geometry, debugging odd behavior.
- Hand routine work to cheaper or faster subagents: UI text, simple scripts, renaming, notes, asset searches, repetitive edits.
- Check spatial results visually (MCP `screen_capture`, or a playtest plus a screenshot). Don't trust coordinates alone.

## Loop
Design (`games/<game>/design.md`) → build in Studio via MCP → playtest (`start_stop_play` + `get_console_output` + `screen_capture`) → fix → save → update `status.md` → publish (`roblox-publishing`).

## Long-term games
Once a game is maintained long-term, spin it off into its own dedicated bot (and maybe its own repo). Copy its `games/<game>/` notes and any game-specific skills there.
# Sugarloaf Canyon: Theme Bible

VR mini golf course for Roblox. **18 holes, total par 53** (front 26, back 27; planned 54, H15 plays as a par 3, see `playtest-log.md`). It's a candy-striped desert canyon at sunset: stacked
sandstone mesas banded pink and cream, rock-candy crystals growing out of the cliffs, saguaros wearing swirl lollipops and gumball fruit,
rope bridges, a peppermint dam with a 91-stud spillway, and Old Sugarloaf, a lone striped dome on the skyline.
Reference art: `../store/ui/card_soon_sugarloafcanyon_color_clean.png` (menu card).

Status: **all 18 holes specced and sim-tuned.** Hole geometry is in `holes.json` (generated by `tools/build_holes.py`), with per-hole
`hNN-notes.md` and `hNN-layout.png`, `course-map.png`, `holes_layouts_sheet.jpg`, and every tuning round in `playtest-log.md`. The world
layout is in `world/world.json` (placements, footprints, walkways), checked in `world/validation.md` (CLEAN, including "every holes.json
felt and rail corner lies inside its footprint"). Next: Studio build and calibration of the five moving obstacles and the lift.

Units: studs. **1 stud = 0.3 m**, Y up, +X east, -Z north. Physics is the same as Lantern Grove (see `tools/README.md`): g 32.7,
rolling deceleration 0.5 + 0.25·v, rails 0.63, bumpers 0.65, cup capture < 5.33 studs/s, cup radius 0.5, ball radius 0.08,
max putt 19.78 studs/s (60 studs on flat felt). Lanes 7-8 wide, greens 12-18, rails 1 high and 0.5 thick.

## Mood

It's warm, golden and a bit silly, like a Wild West postcard drawn by a candy maker. The rock is real-looking sandstone, but its strata run in
peppermint and taffy colours. Crystals are rock candy, cacti carry lollipops, the hoodoos wear gumdrop caps and the dunes are powdered sugar.
Nothing is dangerous: water is clear turquoise, drops have rope railings, and the old mine and ranger station feel like a friendly theme park.
**Scale sells it in VR.** Slot walls rise 44-52 studs (13-16 m) on either side of you, mesa cliffs are 34-50 studs tall, and Old Sugarloaf
stands 190 studs (57 m) above the canyon floor. The player starts at the bottom of the canyon and ends on top of the highest mesa
watching the sun set inside a far-off natural arch.

## World arc (6 zones, low to high)

| Zone | Holes | Ground y | Where | Feel |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Sugarloaf Junction** | H1-H3 | 18 (canyon floor) | South-centre, both banks of the Sugarloaf River | Frontier boardwalk town, mine-head, ranger station. Teaches banks, route choice and water |
| **Rock Candy Narrows** | H4-H6 | 18 / 22 / 26 | A slot canyon cut into Taffy Mesa | Enclosed, cool and echoing. Glittering crystal walls, a ribbon of pink sky. First timing obstacle |
| **Taffy Mesa** | H7-H9 | 70 | First mesa top, east | Big open sky again. Rope bridges over slots, gumdrop boulders, a windpump |
| **Sugarloaf Dam** | H10-H12 | 116 (Reservoir Bench) | North: the reservoir and Peppermint Dam | Engineering whimsy: sluices, locks, an intake tower. Walk across the dam crest past the spillway |
| **Hoodoo Garden** | H13-H15 | 150 (Hoodoo Bench) | North-west third tier | Striped hoodoo spires, the old ore cableway, powdered-sugar dunes |
| **Sugarloaf Crown** | H16-H18 | 190 (top tier) | South-west, the highest mesa | Summit finale: sinkhole, sundial, Sunset Arch. Sunset Gondola back to town |

The route goes up in steps: floor (18) → slot (26) → mesa (70) → bench (116) → hoodoo bench (150) → crown (190). Each step up is one
lift ride with a teleport alternative. The views open up with height: from the Crown you can see every earlier zone. The Sunset Gondola
then carries the player 172 studs down to the plaza where they started, so the course is a loop.

## Materials (Roblox)

- **Felt**: SmoothPlastic or Fabric with a subtle noise texture, no reflectance. Inside the Narrows use the sage felt so the slot reads cooler.
- **Rails**: visual meshes are chunky pink sandstone blocks with timber caps. Collision is invisible parts from holes.json, exactly as in Lantern Grove.
- **Terrain**: Sandstone for cliffs and mesa tops, Sand on the canyon floor and dunes, Ground for walkways. The candy stripes are a cliff
  **dressing kit** of striped rock-face meshes placed over the terrain cliffs, banded by world y every 6 studs using strata colours 1-6 in order.
  Painting by height gives you continuous stripes across every cliff.
- **Rock candy**: Glass, Transparency 0.25, Reflectance 0.1, with a faint Neon inner core so the crystals glow at sunset.
- **Water**: Terrain water, colour `#3FB7C9`, WaterTransparency 0.6. The spillway and falls are Beams with foam particles.
- **Peppermint dam and sluices**: SmoothPlastic red and white stripes, concrete texture on the faces.
- **Lollipops and gumballs**: SmoothPlastic, slightly glossy (Reflectance 0.05).

## Lighting (sunset, launch)

- Lighting.ClockTime ~18.4 and GeographicLatitude tuned so the **sun sits at bearing ~262° (just south of west), elevation ~6°**. That's the
  world.json `sun` block. Check it in Studio and adjust latitude rather than moving holes.
- Ambient `#7A4A50`, OutdoorAmbient `#C9806A`, Brightness 2.0, ColorShift_Top `#FFC38A`, ColorShift_Bottom `#B45A7A`.
- Atmosphere: Density 0.35, Offset 0.1, Color `#F7B48E`, Decay `#B4699A`, Glare 0.6, Haze 2.0 (lilac distance, like the card).
- Bloom 0.7 / threshold 1.3, SunRays 0.12. Soft long shadows; the slot canyon floors are in shadow, lit by bounce light only (warm pink).
- Crystals and lantern-cacti PointLights are subtle (brightness 0.5). A later **night variant** (blue-violet sky, glowing crystals, star field) can reuse them.

## Landmarks

1. **Old Sugarloaf**: a free-standing striped dome in the canyon, 144 studs across, summit y ~208. It's the skyline anchor and visible from
   every tee except the three inside the slot canyon (validated).
2. **Peppermint Dam and Spillway Falls**: a red-and-white arched dam holding Sugarloaf Reservoir (water y 108). A 20-wide spillway sheet
   drops 91 studs into the gorge. You walk across its crest between H11 and H12.
3. **Sugarloaf Crown**: the top mesa tier (y 190) with **Sunset Arch** on its west rim and a giant **sundial**.
4. **Rock Candy Narrows**: a slot canyon with crystal-studded walls, 44 wide and 44-52 deep.
5. **Junction town**: false-front shops, a saloon-style clubhouse, a candy-striped water tower and the mine-head.
6. **Licorice Falls** (45 → 16) and **Fizzwater Falls** (148 → 114): ribbons of water off the mesa edges.
7. **Far horizon**: lilac distant mesas, a huge natural arch on the west horizon (the sun sets inside it as seen from the Crown), and slow
   candy-striped hot-air balloons.

## Set pieces (between holes)

- **Ranger Footbridge** (54 long) over the river, H2 → H3.
- **Rock Candy Lift** (+44): an open mine-cage up the slot wall onto Taffy Mesa.
- **Licorice Rope Bridge** (54 long, 26 above the creek), H8 → H9.
- **Taffy Cliff Lift** (+46) onto the Reservoir Bench.
- **Dam crest walk** (92 long, 102 above the gorge), with the spillway roaring beside you.
- **Gumdrop Lift** (+34) onto the Hoodoo Bench, and **Crown Lift** (+40) onto the Crown.
- **Sunset Gondola** (−172 over 682 studs): a seated cabin ride from Sunset Point back to Junction Plaza.

## The 18 holes (concepts)

Each hole has one headline gimmick and none repeats. Footprints (W x L studs) are reserved in world.json; pars are design targets that
the sim will confirm later.

| # | Hole | Zone | Par | Footprint | Gimmick (one line) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Trailhead Gate | Sugarloaf Junction | 2 | 18 x 46 | **Bank shot**: a striped boulder's 45° bank face turns the ball onto an offset green |
| 2 | Mine-Cart Junction | Sugarloaf Junction | 3 | 26 x 58 | **Tunnel choice**: three mine tunnels: short and tight, long and safe, or a dead end that spits the ball back |
| 3 | Ranger's Riverbend | Sugarloaf Junction | 3 | 26 x 76 | **River crossing**: a rail-less log plank (risky) or the railed footbridge (safe); misses respawn at the bridge entry |
| 4 | Crystal Corridor | Rock Candy Narrows | 2 | 20 x 48 | **Pinball crystals**: static flat-faced rock-candy bumpers bounce the ball down the slot |
| 5 | Echo Slot | Rock Candy Narrows | 3 | 24 x 64 | **Half-pipe**: an S-bend U-channel with banked felt walls; ride high to carry the bend |
| 6 | Flash Flood | Rock Candy Narrows | 3 | 24 x 64 | **Timing gate**: a sluice gate slides across the slot, pauses closed, then opens (slide with dwell) |
| 7 | Rope Bridge Run | Taffy Mesa | 4 | 30 x 78 | **Rope-bridge spans**: two railed bridge spans over Taffy Gap with an island between; falls respawn at the bridge entry |
| 8 | Gumdrop Gulch | Taffy Mesa | 3 | 26 x 66 | **Rolling dips**: three gumdrop humps and hollows made of opposing planar slopes |
| 9 | Lollipop Windmill | Taffy Mesa | 3 | 26 x 60 | **Vertical rotor**: a windpump with lollipop vanes turning across the lane |
| 10 | Sluice Chute | Sugarloaf Dam | 2 | 20 x 50 | **One-way slope**: a steep sluice chute drops 4 into a basin green, with felt arrows and chevrons |
| 11 | Floodgate Lock | Sugarloaf Dam | 3 | 26 x 60 | **Ball elevator**: a canal-lock platform lifts the ball 4 studs and comes back down; roll on while it's down |
| 12 | Intake Tower | Sugarloaf Dam | 4 | 34 x 72 | **Pipe teleport**: sink the ball in the intake grate and it shoots out of an outflow pipe near the green, or play the long shoreline route |
| 13 | Hoodoo Maze | Hoodoo Garden | 3 | 30 x 62 | **Pillar maze**: hoodoo-pillar bumpers make three corridors of different length and risk |
| 14 | Ore Bucket Swing | Hoodoo Garden | 3 | 24 x 60 | **Pendulum**: an ore bucket swings from a cableway trestle across the lane |
| 15 | Sugar Dune | Hoodoo Garden | 3 | 36 x 72 | **Side-slope reading**: a broad dune face tilted across the line (and a little downhill), dropping to a two-tier green (planned par 4; plays as a 3) |
| 16 | Sinkhole Drop | Sugarloaf Crown | 2 | 20 x 50 | **Drop between levels**: putt into a sinkhole in the upper deck and fall 4 into a funnel bowl |
| 17 | Sundial | Sugarloaf Crown | 3 | 30 x 62 | **Horizontal sweeper**: the sundial's shadow arm sweeps slowly round a circular green |
| 18 | Sunset Arch | Sugarloaf Crown | 4 | 36 x 82 | **Finale**: bank round a hoodoo, ramp up under the arch, then a geode-shortcut teleport or the rim route to a cliff-edge green |

**Par spread:** four par 2s (H1, H4, H10, H16), eleven par 3s, three par 4s (H7, H12, H18). Total **53**:
Junction 8, Narrows 8, Taffy Mesa 10, Dam 9, Hoodoo 9, Crown 9. (Planned: H15 as the Hoodoo Garden par 4, total 54. Every par-4 version of
the dune either played under par − 0.7 for good players or lost its ace line, and a par-4 Hoodoo Maze was tried and dropped, so the sim's
par of 3 stands. See `playtest-log.md` Round 4.)

**Gimmick families**, so the variety is visible:
- static geometry: bank (H1), crystal bumpers (H4), half-pipe (H5), rolling dips (H8), pillar maze (H13), side slope (H15);
- level changes: one-way chute (H10), lock elevator (H11), sinkhole drop (H16);
- route choice: tunnels (H2), river plank vs bridge (H3), pipe teleport vs shoreline (H12);
- timing obstacles, each with a different motion: slide with dwell (H6), vertical rotor (H9), pendulum (H14), horizontal sweeper (H17);
- spans: rope bridges (H7);
- finale: multi-gimmick (H18).

## Difficulty curve

- **H1-H3 (learn)**: one idea each: bank, route choice, water with a forgiving respawn. Par 2-3, wide felt (8), flags visible from every tee.
- **H4-H6 (read)**: angles and walls in the enclosed slot. The first timing obstacle is H6, which is slow with long, readable pauses.
- **H7-H9 (commit)**: the first par 4 with bridge spans, then pace control (dips) and a second timing obstacle (windpump).
- **H10-H12 (systems)**: one-way chute, lock elevator and pipe teleport, which teach that the course itself moves the ball.
- **H13-H15 (precision)**: maze lines, pendulum timing and side-slope reading: three demanding par 3s.
- **H16-H18 (payoff)**: a short, dramatic par 2, a sweeper green and a showpiece finale. It should feel grand but not punishing (target
  good-player average at or under par + 0.7).

Same sim targets as Lantern Grove: good HIO 0.2-10% with a brute-force ace line on every hole; good average between par − 0.7 and par + 1;
skill gap (average − good) ≥ 0.3; average ≤ par + 1.5; stroke cap ≤ 1%. Timing obstacles block the intended line in 25-50% of phases
with 0 traps, aiming the sim ~10 points above the in-engine target until Studio calibrates it.

## VR comfort

- **No forced locomotion.** Every walkway is optional to walk; teleport points sit at every zone hub (`world.json teleport_points`: Junction Plaza,
  Narrows Mouth, Taffy Lift Top, Dam East Overlook, Hoodoo Lift Top, Crown Lift Top) and the hole-to-hole "next tee" teleport stays available.
- **Lifts and gondola**: constant slow speed (lift 4 studs/s = 1.2 m/s, gondola 6 studs/s), no acceleration jolts, solid floor and railings,
  the horizon visible throughout, and a "teleport instead" button at both ends. The player never moves while turning.
- **Rails at drops**: the validator puts a railing on any walkway side where the ground falls more than 4 studs within 2.5 studs of its
  edge, and on every bridge and the dam crest. Flagged segments are `rails` in world.json.
- **Gentle walking**: ramps ≤ 10%, stairs ≤ 0.8 rise with 1.5 treads, ≤ 10 steps per flight, 4-stud landings.
- **Sun not in your eyes**: the sun is at bearing 262 and 6° up. No tee plays within 45° of it: every hole plays north, south or east.
  The sunset is a side or back view while putting and the set-piece view from greens and overlooks (validated per hole).
- **Big drops are framed, not looked down into from the tee**: H10's chute and H16's sinkhole are 4 studs, with felt arrows. The 91-stud
  spillway is seen from the railed dam crest, not from a tee.
- **Slot canyon light**: keep the Narrows floor bright enough (bounce light, glowing crystals) so the ball is always readable.
- **Head clearance**: nothing hangs lower than 8 studs (2.4 m) over any felt or walkway except the moving obstacles themselves, which
  stay below 4 studs.
- **Flags**: 7.5-stud flag poles on every cup. H5 (half-pipe bend) and H13 (maze) may hide the flag by design; give them a lantern or crystal cue over the green.

## Build notes for the builder

- Place holes with `holeRoot = CFrame.new(origin) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(yaw_deg), 0)` from world.json (= `world_offset` / `world_yaw` in
  holes.json). Build collision straight from holes.json (invisible, Anchored, CanCollide; felt `center` = top surface).
- Terrain: see `world/README.md` (heightmap mapping, region order, water levels, lifts).
- Moving parts: H6 sluice gate, H9 windpump, H11 lock gate + lift platform, H14 ore bucket, H17 sundial arms. Collision parts must be **CanCollide and moved
  every frame** (CFrame per Heartbeat or a constraint), following holes.json motion profiles including `dwell` pauses.
# Sugarloaf Canyon: world layout (concept stage)

One continuous canyon world for the 18 Sugarloaf Canyon holes, built as stacked mesa tiers joined by walkways, stairs, rope bridges, lifts
and a gondola. Everything here is generated from `world.json`, and `world.json` is generated by `tools/build_world.py`. The tools are adapted
from `../../lantern-grove/world/tools/`. All 18 holes are now specced in `../holes.json` (status `specced`); `build_world.py` reads each hole's real cup position and par from it, and `validate.py` checks that every holes.json felt and rail corner lies inside the hole's concept pads.

**World:** terrain 1280 x 1280 studs (384 x 384 m), x -640..640, z -640..640, heights 11..207. Backdrop pieces go out to r ~1500 and the
sky to 2400. **Walk from spawn to the H18 cup: 2687 studs (806 m)**, plus 4 lifts (+164 total) and the Sunset Gondola home (−172).
Validation: **CLEAN**, with 3 expected warnings (the slot-canyon tees can't see Old Sugarloaf). See `validation.md`.

## Conventions

- **Units/axes**: studs, **1 stud = 0.3 m**, +Y up, +X east, **-Z north** (map up). Bearings are compass degrees (0 = north = -Z, 90 = east = +X).
  These are the same axes as Lantern Grove's holes.json and world.json.
- **Hole placement** (`holes[].placement = {origin:[x,y,z], yaw_deg}`): apply to every local point of the hole
  ```
  x' =  lx*cos(yaw) + lz*sin(yaw) + origin.x
  z' = -lx*sin(yaw) + lz*cos(yaw) + origin.z
  y' =  ly + origin.y            piece_yaw_world = piece.yaw + yaw
  ```
  This is a rotation about +Y (positive = counter-clockwise on the map), then a translation. In Roblox:
  `holeRoot = CFrame.new(origin) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(yaw_deg), 0)`, `part.CFrame = holeRoot * localCFrame`, and axes rotate only.
  The local origin is **the tee**, and play runs toward **local -Z**: yaw 0 plays north, 180 south, -90 east and 90 west (no hole plays west, because the sun is there).
- **Footprints** (`holes[].concept`; `holes[].status` = `specced` once holes.json has the hole, else `concept_footprint`):
  - `pads`: flat boxes in the hole's local frame: `{id, x:[x0,x1], z:[z0,z1], y}` (y = offset from origin.y). A single pad spans local
    z +6 (tee standing room) to -L. Holes with a level change have two tiers (H10 and H16 drop 4; H11 rises 4).
  - `spans`: local z ranges where the hole bridges a gap or river, so the ground stays natural (H3 over the Sugarloaf River, H7 over Taffy Gap).
  - `cup_local`: an approximate cup, used as the start of the walkway to the next tee.
  - **Rule for the hole designer:** every felt piece, rail and near-field prop of holes.json must fit inside the hole's pads
    (spans excepted), with the tee at the local origin.
- **Terrain** is a deterministic heightfield `h(x,z)` (`tools/worldlib.py height_at`), evaluated in the order written in `terrain.method`:
  1. base;
  2. regions in list order: `set` (mesa tiers: polygon/ellipse with a cliff falloff), `hill` (Old Sugarloaf, knolls), `slot` (slot canyons carved along a polyline);
  3. rim;
  4. hole pads;
  5. walkway grading;
  6. pad clamp;
  7. water carve.

  Stream and lake banks only carve out to `bank_reach`. An unbounded bank cone once shaved Old Sugarloaf down to y 86; see validation iteration 1.
- **Elevation tiers**: canyon floor 18 · slot floors 18/22/26 · Taffy Mesa + West Ledge 70 · buttes 96/104 · Reservoir Bench 116 (water 108) ·
  Hoodoo Bench 150 · Sugarloaf Crown 190 · Old Sugarloaf summit ~208.
- **Strata** (visual): cliffs are banded every 6 studs of world y in the 6 `terrain.strata.colors`, cycling. Use the band index `floor(y/6) mod 6`
  to pick the striped rock-face kit piece. Then every cliff's stripes line up across the world.

## Files

| File | What it is | How to use it |
|---|---|---|
| `world.json` | Source of truth: bounds, sun, spawn, zones, 18 concept holes, terrain recipe, streams, reservoir, dam, waterfalls, paths, lifts, teleport points, POIs, backdrop pieces | Read with code. Everything else is generated from it |
| `heightmap.png` | 16-bit grayscale, 1280x1280, **1 px = 1 stud** | Pixel (col i, row j) centre = (x = -640 + i + 0.5, z = -640 + j + 0.5); row 0 = north edge. **y = gray/65535 x 260** |
| `heightmap-8bit.png` | 8-bit preview (y = gray/255 x 260, about 1 stud per level) | Preview or importers that need 8-bit |
| `world-map.png` | Annotated plan: terrain tiers with candy strata on cliffs, water, waterfalls, walkways (stairs as ticks), bridges, lifts, gondola, numbered hole footprints with par and pad y, zones, landmarks, teleports, sun arrow, scale, north | For people and planning |
| `world-map-base.png` | Same plan, shapes only, no text | Reference for concept-art image generation |
| `validation.md` / `validation_latest.json` | Log of every validator run / the latest result | - |
| `tools/worldlib.py` | Transforms, concept pads, the heightfield | Shared library |
| `tools/build_world.py` | Authoring script: hole table, placements, terrain regions, water, paths, lifts, scenery | Edit here, then regenerate |
| `tools/validate.py` | Checks (list below); `python3 tools/validate.py "note"` appends an iteration | Exit 0 = clean |
| `tools/make_maps.py` | Writes the PNGs | - |

Regenerate: `cd world && python3 tools/build_world.py && python3 tools/validate.py "what changed" && python3 tools/make_maps.py` (about 15 s).

Spawn: `[75, 18, 505]` in Junction Plaza, facing H1's tee.

## Water

- **Sugarloaf River**: polyline from the dam's plunge pool (0, -298) south past the town to the south edge. Level 16.8 → 14.0, width 14-22,
  bed depth 3, banks rise 0.8 per stud out to 10 studs. Fill Water from the bed to `level`, which is linear between vertices.
- **Licorice Creek** runs west in Licorice Slot (level 46.5 → 45), drops off Taffy Mesa as **Licorice Falls** (45 → 16.5) and crosses the
  floor to join the river (16.0).
- **Fizzwater Creek** crosses the Hoodoo Bench (148.6 → 148), drops as **Fizzwater Falls** (148 → 113.6), then a short cascade into the reservoir.
- **Sugarloaf Reservoir**: ellipse centre (0, -425), radii 190 x 112, water y 108, 4-stud beach. It's held at its south tip by **Peppermint Dam**:
  x -44..44, centre line z -305, 10 thick, crest y 118 with the walkway at 116. The dam spans the notch in the bench edge. **Spillway Falls**:
  a 20-wide sheet from 108 to 16.8 (drop 91) into the river's plunge pool.

## Walkways, lifts, teleports

Each walkway runs from the cup to the next tee. It leaves the green over the rail (`on_hole`) and reaches the tee from behind (a walk-in point
3 studs outside the footprint). Segment types: `walk`/`ramp` (≤ 10%), `stairs` (rise 0.8, tread 1.5, ≤ 10 steps per flight, 4-stud landings),
`landing`, `bridge` (railed rope or plank bridge, ≤ 5% slope), `dam_crest`, `lift` (vertical cage, boarding points ≤ 30 apart, rise ≤ 60)
and `gondola`. Width is 6, or 8 for the Junction Walk. **`rails`** on a segment lists the sides that need a railing because the ground drops more than 4 studs within
2.5 studs of the edge. Bridges and the dam crest always have both. The terrain is graded under walk, ramp and stair segments only.

| Lift / ride | Bottom [x,y,z] | Top [x,y,z] | Rise |
|---|---|---|---|
| Rock Candy Lift (H6 → H7) | [298, 26, 178] | [314, 70, 178] | 44 |
| Taffy Cliff Lift (H9 → H10) | [400, 70, -218] | [400, 116, -246] | 46 |
| Gumdrop Lift (H12 → H13) | [-224, 116, -246] | [-254, 150, -246] | 34 |
| Crown Lift (H15 → H16) | [-300, 150, 66] | [-300, 190, 92] | 40 |
| Sunset Gondola (H18 → hub) | [-420, 190, 136] Sunset Point | [130, 18, 540] Junction | −172 over 682 |

Lifts run at a constant 4 studs/s with no jolts, are open-sided cages with railings, and the player stands still. The gondola is a seated, enclosed
cabin at 6 studs/s. Both have a "teleport instead" button. **Teleport points** (`teleport_points`): Junction Plaza, Narrows Mouth,
Taffy Lift Top, Dam East Overlook, Hoodoo Lift Top and Crown Lift Top. Each is on flat ground, ≤ 14 studs from a walkway and clear of every hole.

## Sun

`sun`: bearing 262 (just south of west), elevation 6, ClockTime ~18.4. **Rule:** no tee plays within 45° of the sun bearing. Every hole
plays N, S or E (validated). The sunset is the view from greens, the dam crest and Sunset Point.

## Validation (tools/validate.py)

The validator checks:
- **Footprints**: they don't overlap (≥ 4 studs between pads of different holes). The closest pair is H5-H6 at 7.0.
- **Footprints on flat ground**: each pad is flat after grading (≤ 0.05) and natural cut/fill is ≤ 6. The worst is H7 at 5.8, at the Taffy Gap rims. Pads aren't
  under water, river carving only happens inside declared spans, and each span really spans something (H3: 6 studs over the river bed; H7: 30 over Taffy Gap).
- **Walkway structure**: walkways are continuous, start at the cup and end at the tee, with the walk-in point ≤ 6 from the footprint.
- **Walkway limits**: grades, stairs, flights and landings; lift rise and offset; lift, gondola, bridge and dam-crest ends on terrain; walkway surface within 0.75 of the terrain.
- **Walkway clearance**: no water without a bridge, ≥ width/2 + 1 from other holes, never crossing its own hole mid-walk, and **railings wherever the ground drops > 4 studs**.
- **View**: the sun isn't in the eyes at any tee, and **Old Sugarloaf is visible from every tee**. The 3 slot-canyon tees are a warning, because they're enclosed by design.
- **Rest of the world**: total walk < 3000; teleport points are valid; scenery stays clear of the footprints; streams flow downhill; the reservoir rim is ≥ water level apart from the dam and the creek mouth; spawn is on the terrain.

All are listed in `world.json backdrop.pieces` (id, kind, pos = base centre, size [x,y,z] in studs, yaw). They're decoration only and
non-colliding.

## Terrain job list (for the builder)

1. Clear x -640..640, z -640..640, y -10..270.
2. **Heightfield**: import `heightmap.png` with region **size (1280, 260, 1280)** and **centre (0, 130, 0)** (min corner (-640, 0, -640), black = y 0, white = y 260),
   or sample `height_at` on a 4-stud grid in a script. Spot-check H1's pad (60, 18, 450) and the Crown pad (-300, 190, 130).
3. Materials: Sand on the floor and dunes, Sandstone on mesa tops, Sandstone with the striped kit on slopes > 40°, Ground on walkways.
4. Water: river, creeks and reservoir to the levels above. Spillway, Licorice and Fizzwater falls are Beams with foam particles.
5. Pads: keep each concept pad flat. Build hole collision from `../holes.json`; near-field props follow its decoration anchors.
6. Walkways: stairs as blocks (tread 1.5), railings on every `rails` side, rope bridges and the dam crest with railings on both sides.
7. Lifts: open cages at the listed points, 4 studs/s; gondola cable from Sunset Point to the plaza; teleport pads at `teleport_points`.
8. Lighting per `../course.md`.

   (`../playtest-log.md`). Validation iterations after that check the real geometry against the footprints.

# Sugarloaf Canyon: Playtest Log

Simulator: `tools/sim.py` (copied from Lantern Grove, physics unchanged). It validates geometry, fuzzes escapes (60k random shots per hole),
brute-forces an ace-line search from the tee, and plays 2000 AI rounds per hole per skill. Physics tests: `tools/test_physics.py`.
Timing obstacles: `tools/timing_check.py` (24 phases per cycle, stop/kick split, 100k-shot soft-lock fuzz). Teleports and the lift:
`tools/teleport_check.py`. Per-round geometry snapshots are in `tools/out/holes-<tag>.json` and raw results in `tools/out/sim-<tag>.json`
(`tools/summarize.py <tag>` prints the tables below and a per-hole target check).

**Physics (the builder's real Roblox values, measured for Lantern Grove; constants in `sim.py` and the `physics` block of `holes.json`):**
1 stud = 0.3 m. g = 32.7 studs/s². Rolling deceleration = 0.5 + 0.25·v studs/s². Rail restitution 0.63, bumper restitution 0.65 (static
bumpers and every moving blocker). Cup radius 0.5 stud, capture speed < 5.33 studs/s (1.6 m/s). Max putt 19.78 studs/s (5.9 m/s) = a
60-stud roll on flat felt. Drops keep 80% of horizontal speed. Pendulum height rule: a swing blocker only stops the ball if its lowest point
over the ball is at or below the ball's centre.

**AI golfers (fixed, same model as Lantern Grove's final rounds).** Per stroke the golfer aims at the furthest target it has line of sight to:
the cup, else the hole's `ai_waypoints` for its current zone. It hits for distance + carry, adding √(2gΔh) for climbs. Gaussian noise:
**good** aim σ 1.0°, power σ 5%, and on 30% of tee shots it goes for the hole's brute-force ace line (most robust grid hit) with line-memory
error ±1.5° / ±8%; **average** aim σ 2.5°, power σ 12%, never goes for aces. The cap is 8 strokes. A hazard costs +1 and resets the ball to
the stroke start, or to the hazard's `respawn` point when it has one. The golfer model was not changed or tuned per hole in any round below;
only the course geometry and the waypoints (where a golfer of that skill would aim) were.

**Flags.** BROKEN = validator error, fuzz/AI escape, or the cup never reached. IMPOSSIBLE = good players capped >50%.
TRIVIAL = HIO >15% or avg < par−1. TOO-HARD = good avg > par+2. WARN:no-HIO-line = the brute-force search found no ace.

**Targets (Lantern Grove's final bands, from the start):**
- good HIO 0.2–10%, with a brute-force ace line on every hole;
- good average within par − 0.7 … par + 1;
- skill gap (average − good) ≥ 0.3;
- average ≤ par + 1.5;
- stroke-cap hits ≤ 1% for both skills;
- validator 0 errors, fuzz 0 escapes, 0 AI escapes, 0 timeouts;
- timing obstacles: the intended line blocked on 25–50% of phases **judged by stops** (Studio estimate = stops + 0.36 kicks, Lantern Grove's
  Round 10 calibration), 0 traps, 0 soft-locks in a 100k-shot fuzz;
- every cup has a flag, and the tee→flag sightline is clear or the hole has a stated visual cue;
- route choices have signs and felt arrows; one-way slopes and drops have felt arrows or chevrons; rail-less crossings respawn at the entry.

Total par 54 (front 26, back 28), as in `course.md`.

## Round 0: tools and sim changes (before any tuning)

| Change | Why |
|---|---|
| `tools/build_holes.py` (adapted from Lantern Grove): all 18 holes, `K()` tuning knobs, `SC_VARIANT` / `SC_OUT` sweep hooks. `world_offset` / `world_yaw` are read from `world/world.json` placements. At the end it checks every felt and wall corner against the hole's `concept.pads` and prints any point outside | Placement and footprint stay consistent with the world layout |
| `geom.py`: `offset_polyline` (mitred channel rails), `octagon_pts`, `bank_wall(ext_bank, ext_back)`, `teleport(half_size, kind, ...)` | H5 half-pipe, H17 round dial, banks near footprint edges, H11 lift |
| `sim.py`: teleports may use a **rectangular trigger** `entry.half_size` (hole-local axes). Physics unchanged | H11 lock chamber: the whole chamber floor is the lift platform |
| `timing_check.py`: `OBSTACLES` filled in for H6, H9, H11, H14 and H17. Every blocked shot is classified as a **stop** (the first contact's velocity change is mostly along the line of travel, \|cos\| ≥ 0.7: a face square to the line) or a **kick** (side face or corner). The table reports the Studio estimate stops + 0.36 kicks | Lantern Grove Round 10: the engine mostly shoves kicked balls on through, so obstacles are judged by stops |
| `tools/sweep.py` = Lantern Grove `sweep_r5.py` + `sweep_r7.py` + the stop/kick split: per variant the validator, fuzz, ace search, 2000 plays per skill and (`--timing`) the timing check. Each process writes its own scratch file | Iteration tooling (one shared scratch file once corrupted a parallel batch at Lantern Grove) |
| `summarize.py`: adds skill gap, par-or-better rates, escapes/timeouts and a per-hole target check | Reporting |
| `render_layout.py`: course map drawn over the hill-shaded canyon heightfield with the world.json walkways, lifts and gondola; H5's base plate drawn as rock with the channel as felt | 18 holes spread over 1280 studs |
| `test_physics.py`: unchanged, 13/13 pass | - |

**Problems found:**
- **Escapes (BROKEN) on H2, H6, H11, H12, H18:** every one is a ball riding up a 1.2-tall corner bank and hopping a 1.0-tall rail where the bank's
  backing ends. Lantern Grove met the same thing and used 2.4-tall invisible rail colliders beside banks.
- **The AI got stuck on most new holes (caps 14-95%)** because zone waypoints pointed at spots *inside* their own zone (H12 shoreline, H13, H14,
  H15, H18, H6 overflow): once the ball reached the point it kept tapping it there. On H4 the planner had one waypoint in the middle of the
  slot and fired back at it from beyond.
- **H5 half-pipe: 69% capped and 93 timeouts.** The steep banks (1.0 over 2.0, 27°) turn forward speed into side-to-side rocking, and the
  speed-dependent drag kills it.
- **TRIVIAL:** H8 (good HIO 36%: the dips line up every ball straight at the cup).
- **No ace line:** H2, H3, H5, H7, H11, H15, H18. **Slightly high aces:** H4, H10, H14, H16 (10-12%).
- H1 and H17 already met every target.

## Round 3: sim rule for pinned balls, H5 half-pipe rebuilt (`p3`)

**Sim change (made before tuning, kept fixed afterwards).** Tracing H5's timeouts showed balls frozen in place against the raised end edge of an
overlapping bank: the cliff contact puts the ball back where it was every step while gravity re-adds speed, so the "< 0.4 studs/s" rest rule
never fires and the stroke times out. New rest rule in `sim.py`: **a ball that moved < 0.01 studs in the last 0.5 s is at rest, at any speed.**
It cannot change a Lantern Grove result (those balls always move), H5 random-shot timeouts went 84 -> 0 per 20k, and `test_physics.py` gained
test 14 (rectangular lift trigger) and test 15 (pinned ball rests): **15/15 pass**.

| Hole | Change | Why / sweep evidence (`tools/sweep.py`, 1000-2000 plays per skill) |
|---|---|---|
| H5 | Banks 2.0 wide / 1.0 rise -> **1.5 wide / 0.3 rise** (13°), channel 7 wide. At each bend the bank on the **outside** runs on to the rail mitre and the bank on the **inside stops at its low-edge mitre** | The inside bank used to run back into the previous straight as a raised ridge across the line (the "invisible bump" in the traces). Steep 1.0 / 0.6 / 0.4 rises: 50-96% capped; 0.3: 0% capped |
| H5 | S-bend compacted (54 -> 40 studs of channel), cup (-0.5, -42) | No ace line at 54 studs. Cups (0,-42): HIO 0.3%; (0,-41): 10.9%; **(-0.5,-42): good 2.62, HIO 7.3%, gap +0.71** |

**Every hole meets every target:** good HIO 0.9-9.2% with a brute-force ace line on all 18 holes; good average within par − 0.7 … par + 1
everywhere; skill gap ≥ +0.36 (smallest H8); average ≤ par + 1.30 (worst H2); stroke-cap hits ≤ 0.7% for both skills; 0 validator errors,
0 escapes in 60k fuzz shots per hole and in 72,000 AI plays, 0 timeouts; timing lines blocked 33-46% of phases **by stops** (no kicks), 0 traps,
0 soft-locks in 500,000 fuzz shots; every flag visible from its tee (H5's slot walls are within the clearance, its crystal cue stays as decoration;
H13 has a lantern cue); no teleport or lift exit carries the ball past its cup.

### LG playtest lessons applied

- **Cup visibility:** 7.5-stud flag on every cup and a tee-eye sightline check against rails, felt and prop occluders; 18/18 visible. Visual cues
  over the cup where tall dressing may still hide it (H5 crystal, H13 lantern).
- **Skill over luck:** every timing obstacle has a reliable safe route about a stroke longer (H6 overflow, H9 bypass, H14 side line, H17 either side;
  H11 has a signal lamp), and good golfers use it where it pays.
- **Respawn on misses:** H3's river and H7's gap respawn at the plank / island marker (+1), never back at the tee.
- **Felt arrows on one-way slopes:** chevrons on the H10 chute and deck, the H15 dune lip and fall line, and arrows into every route choice
  (H2, H3, H6, H9, H11, H12, H13, H16, H18) plus route signs.
- **Moving obstacles judged by stops, not kicks:** square faces on every mover (gate board, vane faces, lock gate, bucket foot square to the line,
  sundial arms), pointed ends on the sliding gate, pendulum foot and small swing per Lantern Grove Round 10. Every block on every timing line is a stop.
- **Rails beside banks 2.4 tall** (invisible), **no settling point under a mover**, **teleports on open floor** and exit speeds checked.

### Caveats (what the sim can't settle)

- **Studio calibration needed for all five movers and the lift.** The stop/kick model is calibrated on one Lantern Grove pendulum; Sugarloaf's
  sliding gate, rotors, lock gate and pendulum have no in-engine measurement yet. Ask the builder to fire each timing line (start, aim, speed in
  `timing-p6.json`) at 24-48 phases in Studio and compare blocked counts; first check CanCollide and per-frame motion.
- **H11 lift:** the sim moves the ball instantly to the quay at clamp(0.5·v, 1.5, 7) studs/s. In the game the platform must reproduce this
  (hold, ride 2 s, push the ball out at that speed) and keep the gate up while it rides. The ace needs the maximum 7.0 exit, so the builder's
  push speed should be exact.
- **H12 / H18 teleports** are generous by design (any exit at ≥ 4-4.8 studs/s holes): aces stay rare only because reaching the grate/geode fast is rare.
- **Par 53, not 54:** H15 plays as a par 3 (see Round 4). If the owner wants 54 back, H15 needs a genuinely longer layout (a new footprint), not tuning.
- **Close to the edge:** H5 (6 ace grid hits), H7 (1 ace grid hit: a 0.3°-wide line, so aces are rare in VR too), H8 gap +0.36, H13 good 2.35
  (band starts at 2.3). They meet the targets with the fixed golfer model, but a playtest may move them.
- **H5 base plate:** H5's collision felt is three big plates that run under the slot rock; only the channel between the rails is reachable.
  Hide the rest under the wall meshes.
- Decoration and props aren't modelled for physics; the 3D modeler must keep visuals over the colliders (bumper bases, bucket skid plate,
  vane and arm meshes at their pivots).

# Sugarloaf Canyon: Consolidated Prop List (for 3D Model Bot)

Blender assets for Roblox; **fewer than 20,000 triangles per mesh**. Split large cliffs, buildings and assemblies into reusable meshes. Units are studs (1 stud = 0.3 m), +Y up, +X east, -Z north. Dimensions are [X, Y, Z] unless explicitly labelled as X/Z footprints. All mesh visuals have collision disabled. **Collider dressing** fits the invisible `holes.json` part exactly at felt level; visual-only pieces add no gameplay collision. Moving assemblies need separate meshes with origins at their specified animation pivots.

Priority: **P1** = playable/readable course; **P2** = strong theming; **P3** = polish.

`holes.json` controls hole dimensions, positions, flags, hazards, teleports, colliders and motion. `world/world.json` supplies world-only pieces where no holes.json record exists. Hole coordinates are local; world coordinates are explicitly labelled. Convert using world = R_y(world_yaw) × local + world_offset. Decorative sizes labelled approximate do not override collision. Missing dimensions or motion values remain unspecified rather than invented.

H2, H4, H9, H14 and H17 are excluded while being redesigned. World infrastructure remains in scope, including paths and rides connecting those zones.

Palette: green felt `#4FA83D`, raised felt `#86C24A`, slot felt `#3B7F35`; sandstone rails `#E39A82`, timber `#8A5A33`, rope `#C9A46A`; sand `#D88E5C` / mesa sand `#E59C7C`; peppermint `#D9343A` / `#FFF8F0`; crystals `#FF6FA8` / `#FFB347`; cactus `#3E8E41`, swirl `#F78FB3`, fruit `#FFD84A` / `#9BDB4D` / `#E8304A`; water `#3FB7C9`, foam `#E6FAFF`, teleport glow `#D8FF6A`. **Flags use JSON red `#e03c31`.** Strata cycle every 6 world-Y studs: `#E8736B`, `#F6E3C8`, `#D9534F`, `#F2B48A`, `#C8463F`, `#FBEAD6` (index floor(worldY/6) mod 6). Use sandstone/sand, subtle matte felt, low-gloss candy and glass crystals (Transparency 0.25, Reflectance 0.1, faint Neon core). Sunset gold `#FFD36B`, rose sky `#F7A1A6`, tangerine horizon `#F28C28`, lilac haze `#B49AC9`.
Source paths below use zero-based array indices; IDs/names identify reusable instances. **Visual only** includes trigger/hazard markers: those dress a sensor, not a solid collider. `decoration_anchors[].occluders` are sightline envelopes, not collision. Static mesh origins use the listed base or box centre; felt origins are TOP-surface centres. A sloped felt piece uses its own yawed axes and end heights center.y ± rise/2. Preserve all openings and ball routes. Overhead dressing clears felt/walkways by at least 8 studs; flags and intentional low gameplay cues are separate. Preserve JSON occluder envelopes and tee-to-flag visibility. Rail heights below are collision-box heights, which can extend below felt; do not replace them with generic 1-stud boxes.

## 1) World pieces

All coordinates in this section are **world-space**. World meshes are **visual only; holes.json mapping: none** unless explicitly overridden below. Terrain and player-support collision are a builder responsibility from `world/world.json`; they are not new golf colliders. Cut terrain/dressing away from the authoritative hole surfaces, low greens, open bridge gaps and H16 underpass. World rides remain included even when they connect a redesign hole.

## 3) Moving parts

Every moving contact assembly is a separate mesh rooted at its animation pivot; the invisible blocker is moved by the builder. Blocker centres below are offsets from that pivot at offset/angle zero. A decorative base-centre anchor is not automatically a hinge or axle. No rotating/swinging gameplay parts remain among the 13 active holes; H6 and H11 are sliders, plus H11’s translating/tilting raft. Visual effects, facing flags and unspecified mechanical accessories are listed separately below.

## Mismatches

**holes.json values were used wherever a hole has an authored value**, including exact contact geometry over approximate decorative envelopes. World-only pieces use world/world.json with the explicit exceptions/ambiguities below. No disagreements confined to H2, H4, H9, H14 or H17 are listed. Historic playtest rounds describe superseded designs; Round6 and the current JSON govern this handoff. Numeric conflicts within holes.json itself are also exposed rather than silently “fixed”.

- **Flags / palette:** `course.md` assigns peppermint red `#D9343A` to flags; every active `cup.flag.banner_color` is `#e03c31`. Use the JSON colour, pole diameter 0.15, height 7.5 and banner [2,1.2,0.05]. Anchor envelope [2,7.5,0.15] is not the banner thickness.
- **Generic rails / felt widths:** theme prose says rails 1 high and lanes 7–8 wide. Exact JSON rails include below-felt depth and taller safety boxes (e.g. H6 2.9-high boxes reach 2.4 above felt; H10 chute boxes are 5.1 high); H7 lane is 10 wide, H3 plank is 2, H12 pier is 3. Use each collider and felt row. Historical playtest advice to keep visual rails 1 high concerns the visual cap, not the invisible collision envelope.
- **H1 arch:** anchor prose says crossbeam bottom Y8, while `decoration_anchors[1].occluders[2]` centre Y9.25 and height1.5 imply bottom Y8.5. Use the JSON occluder envelope for the crossbeam and retain ≥8 head clearance.
- **H3 ranger station:** notes/hole anchor specify [10,18,10] at local [−19,0,−20] = world [−75,18,311]. World `backdrop.pieces[32]` instead gives [18,16,14] at [−135,18.4,372]; the world POI is [−135,18,372]. Use the hole anchor for the one station; do not build a duplicate at the old POI. “West of shore” is local descriptive language, not an alternative world transform.
- **H3 river height:** `hazards[0]` is local Y−1.5 = world Y16.5 at X−54,Z330. The world river interpolates approximately Y14.91 at Z330 (between Z300/Y15 and Z400/Y14.7). Use Y16.5 and the 26 ×22 hazard footprint at the crossing; blend world-only water away from play rather than lowering the hole hazard.
- **H3 bridge envelopes:** peeled-log dressing [2.6,1.2,22] and safe bridge [6,3,22] include timber/rails; their actual felt widths are 2 and 5. Do not widen felt to the decorative widths. The 54-long world Ranger Footbridge is a separate between-hole bridge, not either 22-long playable crossing.
- **H5 visibility:** notes’ challenge and `echo_walls` / `crystal_cue` descriptions say the flag is hidden by design, but the notes’ own final sightline is visible (+4.90), and Round6 says all flags visible. Keep the crystal cue and preserve visibility; do not enlarge the wall occluders to hide the flag. Use current `plate_visible[0]` and ten 1.5-wide/0.3-rise banks, not earlier 54-long/steep-bank versions in historical log rounds.
- **H6 gate dimensions and pivot:** props prose and decoration envelope say [7,1.2,0.6] and “gate centre”; `moving_parts[0].visual` says [7,1,0.6]. The exact animation pivot is [0,0,−28] at the bottom centre; main blocker centre is [0,0.5,0] relative to it, not coincident with it. Use exact blocker geometry (6.4 body plus two 0.4243-square yaw45 tips) and 1.0 contact height.
- **H6 dwell:** intended-shot prose says closed1.5 s; the exact motion block and latest playtest say closed1.2 s, open2.0 s, speed3, range[−7,0], period7.8667. The JSON values were used, including phase0 (start of the closed pause).
- **H7 respawn:** the theme concept table says falls respawn at bridge entry; current `hazards[0].respawn` is [0,0,−38.5] on the island. Use that marker, not the tee-side bridge1 entry.
- **H7 group bounds and open edges:** `rope_bridges` envelope [8,4,32] at [0,0,−42] does not locate both current decks: bridge2 is centred X3.8 and reaches X5.8. Use 4 ×11 at [0,0,−31.5] and 4 ×13 at [3.8,0,−51.5] plus exact rope rails. `island_edge_lip` says open ends but anchors a long strip at Z−37, where the south rails/bridge mouth are; the open ends are X±9. Keep those ends open, preserve north/south rails, and do not create a visual barrier across the bridge mouth.
- **H8 hump count and bumper shape:** theme concept says three humps; JSON `dip1..dip8`, the current notes and latest log give four (peaks+0.5, hollows−0.3). “Octagon-ish” gumdrop prose and the shared anchor [0,0,−50] are decorative: the two exact colliders are square boxes [1.8,2,1.8], yaw22.5°, centred [1.5,0.5,−51] and [−3.5,0.5,−54]. Use these two contact footprints.
- **H10 chute / terrain / walkway:** theme and world README say drop4; world `holes[9].concept` lower pad is localY−4 and worldY112. JSON chute drop is3.6, cup localY−3.6 = world [283,112.4,−266.5]. `paths[10] (p_h10_h11)` starts at [283,112,−266.5], 0.4 too low. Use the JSON cup/felt heights for the on-hole walkway and flume, with a transition into the existing world route. The world upper pad extends to Z−22 while the chute already slopes from Z−14: carve terrain below the sloping felt instead of filling it with the flat pad.
- **H10 basin wall / dam cue:** `basin_bank` at [0,−3.6,−40] overlaps the banked felt `basin_back`; the real back boundary is `walls[6] (rail7)` at [0,−2.55,−43.25], size[0.5,1.5,19], yaw90. Fit wall cladding to that boundary. `dam_view` local[0,0,−60] transforms to [290,116,−240] and carries approximate [80,100,10], whereas the actual world dam is [88,101,10] based at [0,17,−305]. Interpret `dam_view` as its name indicates—a view cue—while retaining the single world dam; no hole dam collider exists.
- **H11 gate/platform synchronization:** the gate profile closes for2.8 s, while `teleports[0].ride.motion` asks for a2 s rise, release tilt, then1.5 s descent before the gate sinks (at least3.5 s). Those instructions cannot both fit that dwell. Both JSON profiles are transcribed exactly; no fabricated timing correction was made. Gate cycle remains9.6 s, and exact raft phase/tilt duration must be resolved by the builder before animation. The platform top centre [0,0,−23] differs from sensor centre [0,0,−23.25]; no tilt hinge is supplied. Keep these distinct.
- **H11 clearance / chamber:** the theme’s blanket ≥8-stud overhead clearance does not hold for the anchored signal lamp at [0,5,−25.4] over the Y4 quay (lamp base only1 above that felt). Preserve its JSON position/envelope as an explicit low gameplay cue; do not treat it as an overhead walkway fixture. Chamber envelope [4.2,4,5] is scenic, not the raft size or the 2.9-high wall boxes. Mirrored east winch is described but only west X−2.6 is anchored; X+2.6 is a derived companion, not another stored anchor.
- **H12 intake tower:** hole notes/anchor give [7,16,7] at local[6,−1.2,−38] = world[−162,114.8,−264]. World `backdrop.pieces[31]` gives [14,26,14] at [−120,100,−345], and POI Y108. Use the hole landmark and do not instantiate a conflicting duplicate.
- **H12 reservoir / pier:** local hazard is 25 ×28 at [1.5,−1.2,−36] = world[−164,114.8,−268.5], whereas the world reservoir water is Y108, ellipse centred[0,−425] with radii[190,112]. The local pool is both higher and outside that ellipse. Use the JSON pool around play and connect it scenically; do not substitute the distant reservoir surface. Pier-post group [3.2,1.4,13] at Z−28 differs from the exact 3 ×13 top centred Z−28.5; align its deck to the felt. Grate mesh diameter1.4 is distinct from trigger diameter0.6.

Use all eight exact faces and five bumper bases. Cue prose suggests a hidden flag, but final sightline is visible (+5.50); retain lantern without introducing an occlusion.

- **H15 terrain / walkway / tier:** world single pad is localY0 and `paths[15] (p_h15_h16)` starts [−306,150,−58]; actual cup is local[6,−4.6,−57] = world[−306,145.4,−58], 4.6 lower. # Hole 1: Trailhead Gate (Par 2)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Junction**. Gimmick: **Bank shot: a striped boulder's 45-degree face turns the ball east onto an offset green**.
Layout: `h01-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[0]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [60.0, 18.0, 470.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [6.0, 0.0, -30.5] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
A candy-striped timber **trailhead arch** spans the lane with a swirl-lollipop sign; at the far end a big pink-and-cream striped **boulder** sits in the back-left corner of the green, its flat south-east face turned 45° to the lane.

## Gimmick
Bank shot: a striped boulder's 45-degree face turns the ball east onto an offset green.

## Intended shots
1. Roll straight up the 7-wide lane into the boulder's 45° face: it turns the ball east along the back of the offset green.
2. The cup sits off that line toward the front of the green, so the second putt comes back toward you (two-putt par).

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (35 grid shots). The most robust: aim **249.5°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (-0.35, -0.94) in x/z, i.e. 20.5° left of straight -Z), speed **19.0 studs/s** (96% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Teaches the bank. Pace matters more than line: too soft and the ball dies on the face, too firm and it runs the length of the green into the east rail. The ace needs a slightly angled tee shot so the bank sends the ball back toward the cup.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- none

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [6.0, 7.5, -30.5]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +5.50 studs).

# Hole 3: Ranger's Riverbend (Par 3)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Junction**. Gimmick: **River crossing: a rail-less log plank straight over the river (risky) or the railed ranger footbridge (safe); a ball in the river respawns at the plank entry**.
Layout: `h03-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[2]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [-95.0, 18.0, 330.0], yaw -90.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [1.0, 0.0, -55.5] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
The **ranger station** on stilts with a fire-lookout cupola watches over the turquoise Sugarloaf River. Two crossings: a rail-less peeled **log plank** straight from the tee, and the railed **ranger footbridge** off to the west.

## Gimmick
River crossing: a rail-less log plank straight over the river (risky) or the railed ranger footbridge (safe); a ball in the river respawns at the plank entry.

## Intended shots
**Risky line:** straight down the 2-wide rail-less plank, 22 studs over the river, to the north bank and the cup (a 55-stud max-power putt can ace it).
**Safe line:** to the footbridge mouth, across the railed bridge, then two putts on the north bank.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (4 grid shots). The most robust: aim **271.0°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (0.02, -1.00) in x/z, i.e. 1.0° right of straight -Z), speed **19.0 studs/s** (96% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Nerve and line. A ball that falls off the plank costs a stroke and **reappears at rest on the glowing marker stone at the plank entry** (not back at the tee). The footbridge is a sure par.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)

# Hole 5: Echo Slot (Par 3)

Zone: **Rock Candy Narrows**. Gimmick: **Half-pipe: an S-bend U-channel with banked felt walls; ride high to carry the bend**.
Layout: `h05-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[4]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [280.0, 22.0, 326.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [-0.5, 0.0, -42.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
Wave-carved **Echo Slot**: smooth pink-and-cream walls curve around an S-bend half-pipe channel whose felt banks rise either side.

## Gimmick
Half-pipe: an S-bend U-channel with banked felt walls; ride high to carry the bend.

## Intended shots
1. Tee shot into the first (right-hand) bend, riding the outside bank round.
2. Up the middle straight and round the second (left-hand) bend.
3. Out of the channel mouth onto the green.
A perfect max-power tee shot rides both banks and carries to the cup.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (6 grid shots). The most robust: aim **288.0°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (0.31, -0.95) in x/z, i.e. 18.0° right of straight -Z), speed **19.8 studs/s** (100% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Ride the banks: pace decides how high the ball rides and where it leaves each bend. The slot walls hide the flag from the tee by design: a glowing rock-candy crystal hangs 9.5 studs over the cup as the cue.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- none

# Hole 6: Flash Flood (Par 3)

Zone: **Rock Candy Narrows**. Gimmick: **Timing gate: a sluice gate slides across the slot neck, pauses closed, then opens (slide with dwell); the overflow channel is the safe way round**.
Layout: `h06-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[5]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [280.0, 26.0, 255.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [-4.0, 0.0, -45.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
An old timber **sluice gate** with peppermint bands and a hand-wheel winch slides across the slot neck; a little **flood-warning bell tower** stands on the rock beside it.

## Gimmick
Timing gate: a sluice gate slides across the slot neck, pauses closed, then opens (slide with dwell); the overflow channel is the safe way round.

## Intended shots
**Timing line:** tee shot through the 6-wide neck while the gate is open (it pauses open 2.0 s, closed 1.5 s), into the green: birdie chance.
**Safe line:** from the basin into the east overflow channel, up its 21 studs to the crossover, then putt: a sure par.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (213 grid shots). The most robust: aim **290.0°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (0.34, -0.94) in x/z, i.e. 20.0° right of straight -Z), speed **19.0 studs/s** (96% power), start phase t=1.5s of the moving parts. The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
First timing obstacle, slow and readable. The gate's pointed ends never bat a ball along its track (Lantern Grove lesson).

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- **sluice_gate** (slide): pivot [0, 0.0, -28.0], axis [1, 0, 0], speed 3.0 studs/s, ping-pong -7.0..0.0 studs with pauses 1.2 s at 0, 2 s at -7, phase 0.0, cycle 7.87 s. Blockers (relative to pivot at angle/offset 0): box [6.4, 1.0, 0.6] at [0, 0.5, 0]; box [0.4243, 1.0, 0.4243] at [-3.2, 0.5, 0] yaw 45.0; box [0.4243, 1.0, 0.4243] at [3.2, 0.5, 0] yaw 45.0. Visual: Timber sluice gate board 7 long, 0.6 thick, 1.0 tall (pointed 45-degree ends) on a hidden rail across the neck; closed = centred on the neck (offset 0), open = slid 7 west into a pocket in the slot wall

## Concept prompt
First-person VR view at eye height in a slot canyon: a felt lane widens into a basin, then narrows to a neck crossed by a sliding timber sluice gate with red-and-white stripes and a big winch wheel; a tiny bell tower on the rock; an overflow channel curving off to the right. Style: candy-striped sunset desert canyon, pink and cream banded sandstone, rock-candy crystals, lollipop saguaros, warm golden low sun from the west, lilac haze, chunky whimsical Roblox-friendly shapes, no text.
# Hole 7: Rope Bridge Run (Par 4)

Zone: **Taffy Mesa**. Gimmick: **Rope-bridge spans: two railed rope bridges over Taffy Gap with an island between whose ends are open to the gap; a fall respawns at the island**.
Layout: `h07-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[6]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [340.0, 70.0, 150.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [3.0, 0.0, -60.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
**Taffy Gap**: two rope bridges with plank decks cross a 30-stud striped chasm, with a hoodoo island between them.

## Gimmick
Rope-bridge spans: two railed rope bridges over Taffy Gap with an island between whose ends are open to the gap; a fall respawns at the island.

## Intended shots
**Normal play:** 1) across bridge 1 to the island; 2) from the island through bridge 2 onto the far green; 3-4) putt.
**Ace line:** a near-max-power putt threads both bridges in one straight line (the bridges are offset so only a 0.3°-wide line runs clean).

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (1 grid shots). The most robust: aim **272.5°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (0.04, -1.00) in x/z, i.e. 2.5° right of straight -Z), speed **19.8 studs/s** (100% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Long and committed. The island's ends are open to the gap: a ball that falls in **respawns at rest on the island marker stone** (+1).

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- **hazard taffy_gap** (pit): +1 stroke, ball placed at rest on the respawn point [0.0, 0.0, -38.5]

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [3.0, 7.5, -60.0]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +5.50 studs).

## Playtest stats (final, p6, 2000 plays per skill)
| Skill | Avg strokes | HIO | Par or better | Capped (8) | Escapes / timeouts | Strokes 1..8 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| good | 3.57 | 1.6% | 90% | 0.0% | 0 / 0 | [32, 131, 780, 856, 126, 69, 5, 1] |
| average | 4.20 | 0.0% | 68% | 0.1% | 0 / 0 | [0, 65, 436, 867, 386, 180, 45, 21] |

## Props for the 3D modeler (decoration only, sizes in studs)
Rope bridges x2 (plank deck, rope hand-lines, posts) (P1, landmark); island hoodoo with gumdrop cap (P2); island respawn marker (P1); crumbly lip trim on the open island ends (P2); flag 7.5 (P1).

Decoration anchors (position in hole-local studs):
- `cup_flag` at [3.0, 0.0, -60.0] size [2.0, 7.5, 0.15]: Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 dia) in the cup, 2.0x1.2 banner at the top, turns to face the tee; no collision
- `rope_bridges` at [0, 0, -42] (LANDMARK) size [8, 4, 32]: Two sagging-look rope bridges (plank decks, rope hand-lines on posts) over Taffy Gap; the felt deck is flat
- `island_hoodoo` at [-6.5, 0, -41] size [3, 12, 3]: Striped hoodoo with a gumdrop cap on the island's west end (outside the play line; decoration only)
- `island_respawn_stone` at [0, 0, -38.5] size [0.8, 0.02, 0.8]: Glowing marker stone flush in the island felt at the bridge-1 landing: where a ball that falls into the gap reappears
- `gap_view` at [0, -30, -42] size [30, 30, 32]: Taffy Gap: 30-stud deep striped slot with a creek far below (seen past the open island edges)
- `island_edge_lip` at [0, 0, -37.0] size [18, 0.2, 0.4]: Low crumbly sandstone lip (0.2, visual only) along the open island edges so they read as edges

## Concept prompt
First-person VR view at eye height on a candy-striped mesa top at sunset: a felt lane runs to a narrow rope bridge over a deep striped chasm, a small rock island with a hoodoo, a second rope bridge beyond and a green with a red flag on the far rim; big open sky. Style: candy-striped sunset desert canyon, pink and cream banded sandstone, rock-candy crystals, lollipop saguaros, warm golden low sun from the west, lilac haze, chunky whimsical Roblox-friendly shapes, no text.
# Hole 8: Gumdrop Gulch (Par 3)

Zone: **Taffy Mesa**. Gimmick: **Rolling dips: gumdrop humps and hollows made of opposing planar slopes; pace the ball through**.
Layout: `h08-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[7]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [360.0, 70.0, 55.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [5.0, 0.0, -55.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
Car-sized sugar-dusted **gumdrops** in cherry, lemon and lime line Gumdrop Gulch; two smaller ones guard the green.

## Gimmick
Rolling dips: gumdrop humps and hollows made of opposing planar slopes; pace the ball through.

## Intended shots
1. Pace the tee shot over the four humps (each rises 0.5 and dips 0.3 below the lane) and onto the green.
2. Putt round the gumdrops to the cup.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (14 grid shots). The most robust: aim **251.0°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (-0.33, -0.95) in x/z, i.e. 19.0° left of straight -Z), speed **19.4 studs/s** (98% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Pace control: too soft settles in a hollow, too firm runs through the green. Aces need exactly the right pace from the tee.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- none

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [5.0, 7.5, -55.0]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +5.44 studs).

## Playtest stats (final, p6, 2000 plays per skill)
| Skill | Avg strokes | HIO | Par or better | Capped (8) | Escapes / timeouts | Strokes 1..8 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| good | 2.41 | 4.9% | 98% | 0.0% | 0 / 0 | [97, 1025, 834, 44, 0, 0, 0, 0] |
| average | 2.78 | 0.1% | 88% | 0.0% | 0 / 0 | [3, 722, 1030, 210, 34, 1, 0, 0] |

## Props for the 3D modeler (decoration only, sizes in studs)
Big gumdrop boulders (P2); 2 gumdrop dressings over the 1.8 bumpers (P1, landmark); taffy-striped rail trim following the humps (P2); flag 7.5 (P1).

Decoration anchors (position in hole-local studs):
- `cup_flag` at [5.0, 0.0, -55.0] size [2.0, 7.5, 0.15]: Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 dia) in the cup, 2.0x1.2 banner at the top, turns to face the tee; no collision
- `gumdrops` at [0, 0, -50.0] (LANDMARK) size [2.4, 2.4, 2.4]: Giant sugar-dusted gumdrops (cherry, lemon, lime) dressing the two gumdrop bumpers on the green (1.8 x 1.8 octagon-ish bases turned 22.5 deg)
- `dip_trim` at [0, 0, -24.0] size [9, 0.4, 32.0]: Taffy-striped rail trim following the 4 humps (felt humps rise to +0.5 and dip to -0.3)
- `big_gumdrop_bg` at [11, 0, -18] size [6, 5, 6]: Car-sized gumdrop boulders outside the east rail
