## Mismatches

**holes.json values were used wherever a hole has an authored value**, including exact contact geometry over approximate decorative envelopes. World-only pieces use world/world.json with the explicit exceptions/ambiguities below. No disagreements confined to H2, H4, H9, H14 or H17 are listed. Historic playtest rounds describe superseded designs; Round6 and the current JSON govern this handoff. Numeric conflicts within holes.json itself are also exposed rather than silently “fixed”.

- **Flags / palette:** `course.md` assigns peppermint red `#D9343A` to flags; every active `cup.flag.banner_color` is `#e03c31`. Use the JSON colour, pole diameter 0.15, height 7.5 and banner [2,1.2,0.05]. Anchor envelope [2,7.5,0.15] is not the banner thickness.
- **Generic rails / felt widths:** theme prose says rails 1 high and lanes 7–8 wide. Exact JSON rails include below-felt depth and taller safety boxes (e.g. H6 2.9-high boxes reach 2.4 above felt; H10 chute boxes are 5.1 high); H7 lane is 10 wide, H3 plank is 2, H12 pier is 3. Use each collider and felt row. Historical playtest advice to keep visual rails 1 high concerns the visual cap, not the invisible collision envelope.
- **H1 arch:** anchor prose says crossbeam bottom Y8, while `decoration_anchors[1].occluders[2]` centre Y9.25 and height1.5 imply bottom Y8.5. Use the JSON occluder envelope for the crossbeam and retain ≥8 head clearance.
- **H3 ranger station:** notes/hole anchor specify [10,18,10] at local [−19,0,−20] = world [−75,18,311]. World `backdrop.pieces[32]` instead gives [18,16,14] at [−135,18.4,372]; the world POI is [−135,18,372]. Use the hole anchor for the one station; do not build a duplicate at the old POI. “West of shore” is local descriptive language, not an alternative world transform.
- **H3 river height:** `hazards[0]` is local Y−1.5 = world Y16.5 at X−54,Z330. The world river interpolates approximately Y14.91 at Z330 (between Z300/Y15 and Z400/Y14.7). Use Y16.5 and the 26 ×22 hazard footprint at the crossing; blend world-only water away from play rather than lowering the hole hazard.
- **H3 bridge envelopes:** peeled-log dressing [2.6,1.2,22] and safe bridge [6,3,22] include timber/rails; their actual felt widths are 2 and 5. Do not widen felt to the decorative widths. The 54-long world Ranger Footbridge is a separate between-hole bridge, not either 22-long playable crossing.
- **H5 visibility:** notes’ challenge and `echo_walls` / `crystal_cue` descriptions say the flag is hidden by design, but the notes’ own final sightline is visible (+4.90), and Round6 says all flags visible. Keep the crystal cue and preserve visibility; do not enlarge the wall occluders to hide the flag. Use current `plate_visible[0]` and ten 1.5-wide/0.3-rise banks, not earlier 54-long/steep-bank versions in historical log rounds.
- **H6 gate dimensions and pivot:** props prose and decoration envelope say [7,1.2,0.6] and “gate centre”; `moving_parts[0].visual` says [7,1,0.6]. The exact animation pivot is [0,0,−28] at the bottom centre; main blocker centre is [0,0.5,0] relative to it, not coincident with it. Use exact blocker geometry (6.4 body plus two 0.4243-square yaw45 tips) and 1.0 contact height.
- **H6 dwell:** intended-shot prose says closed1.5 s; the exact motion block and latest playtest say closed1.2 s, open2.0 s, speed3, range[−7,0], period7.8667. The JSON values were used, including phase0 (start of the closed pause).
- **H7 respawn:** the theme concept table says falls respawn at bridge entry; current `hazards[0].respawn` is [0,0,−38.5] on the island. Use that marker, not the tee-side bridge1 entry.
- **H7 group bounds and open edges:** `rope_bridges` envelope [8,4,32] at [0,0,−42] does not locate both current decks: bridge2 is centred X3.8 and reaches X5.8. Use 4 ×11 at [0,0,−31.5] and 4 ×13 at [3.8,0,−51.5] plus exact rope rails. `island_edge_lip` says open ends but anchors a long strip at Z−37, where the south rails/bridge mouth are; the open ends are X±9. Keep those ends open, preserve north/south rails, and do not create a visual barrier across the bridge mouth.
- **H8 hump count and bumper shape:** theme concept says three humps; JSON `dip1..dip8`, the current notes and latest log give four (peaks+0.5, hollows−0.3). “Octagon-ish” gumdrop prose and the shared anchor [0,0,−50] are decorative: the two exact colliders are square boxes [1.8,2,1.8], yaw22.5°, centred [1.5,0.5,−51] and [−3.5,0.5,−54]. Use these two contact footprints.
- **H10 chute / terrain / walkway:** theme and world README say drop4; world `holes[9].concept` lower pad is localY−4 and worldY112. JSON chute drop is3.6, cup localY−3.6 = world [283,112.4,−266.5]. `paths[10] (p_h10_h11)` starts at [283,112,−266.5], 0.4 too low. Use the JSON cup/felt heights for the on-hole walkway and flume, with a transition into the existing world route. The world upper pad extends to Z−22 while the chute already slopes from Z−14: carve terrain below the sloping felt instead of filling it with the flat pad.
- **H10 basin wall / dam cue:** `basin_bank` at [0,−3.6,−40] overlaps the banked felt `basin_back`; the real back boundary is `walls[6] (rail7)` at [0,−2.55,−43.25], size[0.5,1.5,19], yaw90. Fit wall cladding to that boundary. `dam_view` local[0,0,−60] transforms to [290,116,−240] and carries approximate [80,100,10], whereas the actual world dam is [88,101,10] based at [0,17,−305]. Interpret `dam_view` as its name indicates—a view cue—while retaining the single world dam; no hole dam collider exists.
- **H11 gate/platform synchronization:** the gate profile closes for2.8 s, while `teleports[0].ride.motion` asks for a2 s rise, release tilt, then1.5 s descent before the gate sinks (at least3.5 s). Those instructions cannot both fit that dwell. Both JSON profiles are transcribed exactly; no fabricated timing correction was made. Gate cycle remains9.6 s, and exact raft phase/tilt duration must be resolved by the builder before animation. The platform top centre [0,0,−23] differs from sensor centre [0,0,−23.25]; no tilt hinge is supplied. Keep these distinct.
- **H11 clearance / chamber:** the theme’s blanket ≥8-stud overhead clearance does not hold for the anchored signal lamp at [0,5,−25.4] over the Y4 quay (lamp base only1 above that felt). Preserve its JSON position/envelope as an explicit low gameplay cue; do not treat it as an overhead walkway fixture. Chamber envelope [4.2,4,5] is scenic, not the raft size or the 2.9-high wall boxes. Mirrored east winch is described but only west X−2.6 is anchored; X+2.6 is a derived companion, not another stored anchor.
- **H12 intake tower:** hole notes/anchor give [7,16,7] at local[6,−1.2,−38] = world[−162,114.8,−264]. World `backdrop.pieces[31]` gives [14,26,14] at [−120,100,−345], and POI Y108. Use the hole landmark and do not instantiate a conflicting duplicate.
- **H12 reservoir / pier:** local hazard is 25 ×28 at [1.5,−1.2,−36] = world[−164,114.8,−268.5], whereas the world reservoir water is Y108, ellipse centred[0,−425] with radii[190,112]. # Sugarloaf Canyon: world layout (concept stage)

One continuous canyon world for the 18 Sugarloaf Canyon holes, built as stacked mesa tiers joined by walkways, stairs, rope bridges, lifts
and a gondola. Everything here is generated from `world.json`, and `world.json` is generated by `tools/build_world.py`. The tools are adapted
from `../../lantern-grove/world/tools/`. All 18 holes are now specced in `../holes.json` (status `specced`); `build_world.py` reads each hole's real cup position and par from it, and `validate.py` checks that every holes.json felt and rail corner lies inside the hole's concept pads.

**World:** terrain 1280 x 1280 studs (384 x 384 m), x -640..640, z -640..640, heights 11..207. Backdrop pieces go out to r ~1500 and the
sky to 2400. **Walk from spawn to the H18 cup: 2687 studs (806 m)**, plus 4 lifts (+164 total) and the Sunset Gondola home (−172).
Validation: **CLEAN**, with 3 expected warnings (the slot-canyon tees can't see Old Sugarloaf). See `validation.md`.

## Conventions

- **Units/axes**: studs, **1 stud = 0.3 m**, +Y up, +X east, **-Z north** (map up). Bearings are compass degrees (0 = north = -Z, 90 = east = +X).
  These are the same axes as Lantern Grove's holes.json and world.json.
- **Hole placement** (`holes[].placement = {origin:[x,y,z], yaw_deg}`): apply to every local point of the hole
  ```
  x' =  lx*cos(yaw) + lz*sin(yaw) + origin.x
  z' = -lx*sin(yaw) + lz*cos(yaw) + origin.z
  y' =  ly + origin.y            piece_yaw_world = piece.yaw + yaw
  ```
  This is a rotation about +Y (positive = counter-clockwise on the map), then a translation. In Roblox:
  `holeRoot = CFrame.new(origin) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(yaw_deg), 0)`, `part.CFrame = holeRoot * localCFrame`, and axes rotate only.
  The local origin is **the tee**, and play runs toward **local -Z**: yaw 0 plays north, 180 south, -90 east and 90 west (no hole plays west, because the sun is there).
- **Footprints** (`holes[].concept`; `holes[].status` = `specced` once holes.json has the hole, else `concept_footprint`):
  - `pads`: flat boxes in the hole's local frame: `{id, x:[x0,x1], z:[z0,z1], y}` (y = offset from origin.y). A single pad spans local
    z +6 (tee standing room) to -L. Holes with a level change have two tiers (H10 and H16 drop 4; H11 rises 4).
  - `spans`: local z ranges where the hole bridges a gap or river, so the ground stays natural (H3 over the Sugarloaf River, H7 over Taffy Gap).
  - `cup_local`: an approximate cup, used as the start of the walkway to the next tee.
  - **Rule for the hole designer:** every felt piece, rail and near-field prop of holes.json must fit inside the hole's pads
    (spans excepted), with the tee at the local origin.
- **Terrain** is a deterministic heightfield `h(x,z)` (`tools/worldlib.py height_at`), evaluated in the order written in `terrain.method`:
  1. base;
  2. regions in list order: `set` (mesa tiers: polygon/ellipse with a cliff falloff), `hill` (Old Sugarloaf, knolls), `slot` (slot canyons carved along a polyline);
  3. rim;
  4. hole pads;
  5. walkway grading;
  6. pad clamp;
  7. water carve.

  Stream and lake banks only carve out to `bank_reach`. An unbounded bank cone once shaved Old Sugarloaf down to y 86; see validation iteration 1.
- **Elevation tiers**: canyon floor 18 · slot floors 18/22/26 · Taffy Mesa + West Ledge 70 · buttes 96/104 · Reservoir Bench 116 (water 108) ·
  Hoodoo Bench 150 · Sugarloaf Crown 190 · Old Sugarloaf summit ~208.
- **Strata** (visual): cliffs are banded every 6 studs of world y in the 6 `terrain.strata.colors`, cycling. Use the band index `floor(y/6) mod 6`
  to pick the striped rock-face kit piece. Then every cliff's stripes line up across the world.

## Files

| File | What it is | How to use it |
|---|---|---|
| `world.json` | Source of truth: bounds, sun, spawn, zones, 18 concept holes, terrain recipe, streams, reservoir, dam, waterfalls, paths, lifts, teleport points, POIs, backdrop pieces | Read with code. Everything else is generated from it |
| `heightmap.png` | 16-bit grayscale, 1280x1280, **1 px = 1 stud** | Pixel (col i, row j) centre = (x = -640 + i + 0.5, z = -640 + j + 0.5); row 0 = north edge. **y = gray/65535 x 260** |
| `heightmap-8bit.png` | 8-bit preview (y = gray/255 x 260, about 1 stud per level) | Preview or importers that need 8-bit |
| `world-map.png` | Annotated plan: terrain tiers with candy strata on cliffs, water, waterfalls, walkways (stairs as ticks), bridges, lifts, gondola, numbered hole footprints with par and pad y, zones, landmarks, teleports, sun arrow, scale, north | For people and planning |
| `world-map-base.png` | Same plan, shapes only, no text | Reference for concept-art image generation |
| `validation.md` / `validation_latest.json` | Log of every validator run / the latest result | - |
| `tools/worldlib.py` | Transforms, concept pads, the heightfield | Shared library |
| `tools/build_world.py` | Authoring script: hole table, placements, terrain regions, water, paths, lifts, scenery | Edit here, then regenerate |
| `tools/validate.py` | Checks (list below); `python3 tools/validate.py "note"` appends an iteration | Exit 0 = clean |
| `tools/make_maps.py` | Writes the PNGs | - |

Regenerate: `cd world && python3 tools/build_world.py && python3 tools/validate.py "what changed" && python3 tools/make_maps.py` (about 15 s).

Spawn: `[75, 18, 505]` in Junction Plaza, facing H1's tee.

## Water

- **Sugarloaf River**: polyline from the dam's plunge pool (0, -298) south past the town to the south edge. Level 16.8 → 14.0, width 14-22,
  bed depth 3, banks rise 0.8 per stud out to 10 studs. Fill Water from the bed to `level`, which is linear between vertices.
- **Licorice Creek** runs west in Licorice Slot (level 46.5 → 45), drops off Taffy Mesa as **Licorice Falls** (45 → 16.5) and crosses the
  floor to join the river (16.0).
- **Fizzwater Creek** crosses the Hoodoo Bench (148.6 → 148), drops as **Fizzwater Falls** (148 → 113.6), then a short cascade into the reservoir.
- **Sugarloaf Reservoir**: ellipse centre (0, -425), radii 190 x 112, water y 108, 4-stud beach. It's held at its south tip by **Peppermint Dam**:
  x -44..44, centre line z -305, 10 thick, crest y 118 with the walkway at 116. The dam spans the notch in the bench edge. **Spillway Falls**:
  a 20-wide sheet from 108 to 16.8 (drop 91) into the river's plunge pool.

## Walkways, lifts, teleports

Each walkway runs from the cup to the next tee. It leaves the green over the rail (`on_hole`) and reaches the tee from behind (a walk-in point
3 studs outside the footprint). Segment types: `walk`/`ramp` (≤ 10%), `stairs` (rise 0.8, tread 1.5, ≤ 10 steps per flight, 4-stud landings),
`landing`, `bridge` (railed rope or plank bridge, ≤ 5% slope), `dam_crest`, `lift` (vertical cage, boarding points ≤ 30 apart, rise ≤ 60)
and `gondola`. Width is 6, or 8 for the Junction Walk. **`rails`** on a segment lists the sides that need a railing because the ground drops more than 4 studs within
2.5 studs of the edge. Bridges and the dam crest always have both. The terrain is graded under walk, ramp and stair segments only.

| Lift / ride | Bottom [x,y,z] | Top [x,y,z] | Rise |
|---|---|---|---|
| Rock Candy Lift (H6 → H7) | [298, 26, 178] | [314, 70, 178] | 44 |
| Taffy Cliff Lift (H9 → H10) | [400, 70, -218] | [400, 116, -246] | 46 |
| Gumdrop Lift (H12 → H13) | [-224, 116, -246] | [-254, 150, -246] | 34 |
| Crown Lift (H15 → H16) | [-300, 150, 66] | [-300, 190, 92] | 40 |
| Sunset Gondola (H18 → hub) | [-420, 190, 136] Sunset Point | [130, 18, 540] Junction | −172 over 682 |

Lifts run at a constant 4 studs/s with no jolts, are open-sided cages with railings, and the player stands still. The gondola is a seated, enclosed
cabin at 6 studs/s. Both have a "teleport instead" button. **Teleport points** (`teleport_points`): Junction Plaza, Narrows Mouth,
Taffy Lift Top, Dam East Overlook, Hoodoo Lift Top and Crown Lift Top. Each is on flat ground, ≤ 14 studs from a walkway and clear of every hole.

## Sun

`sun`: bearing 262 (just south of west), elevation 6, ClockTime ~18.4. **Rule:** no tee plays within 45° of the sun bearing. Every hole
plays N, S or E (validated). The sunset is the view from greens, the dam crest and Sunset Point.

## Validation (tools/validate.py)

The validator checks:
- **Footprints**: they don't overlap (≥ 4 studs between pads of different holes). The closest pair is H5-H6 at 7.0.
- **Footprints on flat ground**: each pad is flat after grading (≤ 0.05) and natural cut/fill is ≤ 6. The worst is H7 at 5.8, at the Taffy Gap rims. Pads aren't
  under water, river carving only happens inside declared spans, and each span really spans something (H3: 6 studs over the river bed; H7: 30 over Taffy Gap).
- **Walkway structure**: walkways are continuous, start at the cup and end at the tee, with the walk-in point ≤ 6 from the footprint.
- **Walkway limits**: grades, stairs, flights and landings; lift rise and offset; lift, gondola, bridge and dam-crest ends on terrain; walkway surface within 0.75 of the terrain.
- **Walkway clearance**: no water without a bridge, ≥ width/2 + 1 from other holes, never crossing its own hole mid-walk, and **railings wherever the ground drops > 4 studs**.
- **View**: the sun isn't in the eyes at any tee, and **Old Sugarloaf is visible from every tee**. The 3 slot-canyon tees are a warning, because they're enclosed by design.
- **Rest of the world**: total walk < 3000; teleport points are valid; scenery stays clear of the footprints; streams flow downhill; the reservoir rim is ≥ water level apart from the dam and the creek mouth; spawn is on the terrain.

## Terrain job list (for the builder)

1. Clear x -640..640, z -640..640, y -10..270.
2. **Heightfield**: import `heightmap.png` with region **size (1280, 260, 1280)** and **centre (0, 130, 0)** (min corner (-640, 0, -640), black = y 0, white = y 260),
   or sample `height_at` on a 4-stud grid in a script. Spot-check H1's pad (60, 18, 450) and the Crown pad (-300, 190, 130).
3. Materials: Sand on the floor and dunes, Sandstone on mesa tops, Sandstone with the striped kit on slopes > 40°, Ground on walkways.
4. Water: river, creeks and reservoir to the levels above. Spillway, Licorice and Fizzwater falls are Beams with foam particles.
5. Pads: keep each concept pad flat. Build hole collision from `../holes.json`; near-field props follow its decoration anchors.
6. Walkways: stairs as blocks (tread 1.5), railings on every `rails` side, rope bridges and the dam crest with railings on both sides.
7. Lifts: open cages at the listed points, 4 studs/s; gondola cable from Sunset Point to the plaza; teleport pads at `teleport_points`.
8. Lighting per `../course.md`.

## 2) Per-hole props and dressing

Each active hole has an asset table followed by exact felt/rail dressings. Those geometry rows specify the contact surface to model, not extra duplicate assets: combine them with the named dressing where mapped. Approximate decorative envelopes may flare above play, but every contact-level footprint, position and yaw is authoritative. Slots, bridge undersides, cups and drop openings remain hollow.

h10-notes.md
# Hole 10: Sluice Chute (Par 2)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Dam**. Gimmick: **One-way slope: a steep sluice chute drops 3.6 studs into a basin green; felt arrows and chevrons show it is one-way**.
Layout: `h10-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[9]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [290.0, 116.0, -300.0], yaw 180.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [7.0, -3.6, -33.5] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
A timber **sluice flume** with peppermint-striped gate frames drops the ball 3.6 studs down a 16-long chute into a spillway basin; the Peppermint Dam rises beyond.

## Gimmick
One-way slope: a steep sluice chute drops 3.6 studs into a basin green; felt arrows and chevrons show it is one-way.

## Intended shots
1. Roll into the chute mouth: the ball accelerates down the chute into the basin and climbs its back slope.
2. Putt to the cup on the right side of the basin.

## Hole-in-one line

# Hole 11: Floodgate Lock (Par 3)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Dam**. Gimmick: **Ball elevator: a canal-lock platform lifts the ball 4 studs and comes back down; roll in while the lock gate is down**.
Layout: `h11-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[10]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [150.0, 116.0, -252.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [0.0, 4.0, -43.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
A canal **lock** of peppermint concrete with brass winches, a lock-keeper's hut and a red/green signal lamp lifts the ball 4 studs onto the quay.

## Gimmick
Ball elevator: a canal-lock platform lifts the ball 4 studs and comes back down; roll in while the lock gate is down.

## Intended shots
1. Roll into the lock chamber while the gate is down (it is open 5.5 s, closed 2.8 s while the platform rides). The platform lifts the ball and the sluice rush pushes it out along the quay at half its entry speed (1.5-7 studs/s).
2. Putt on the upper green.
Ace: a firm entry sends the ball out fast enough to roll the 16 studs to the cup.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (45 grid shots). The most robust: aim **269.0°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (-0.02, -1.00) in x/z, i.e. 1.0° left of straight -Z), speed **19.8 studs/s** (100% power), start phase t=0.0s of the moving parts. The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Timing the gate (the green lamp is the cue) and choosing the entry pace, which sets the exit roll.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- **lock_gate** (slide): pivot [0, 0.0, -21.25], axis [0, 1, 0], speed 2.0 studs/s, ping-pong -1.3..0.0 studs with pauses 5.5 s at -1.3, 2.8 s at 0, phase -1.3, cycle 9.60 s. Blockers (relative to pivot at angle/offset 0): box [3.6, 1.2, 0.5] at [0, 0.6, 0]. Visual: Mitre-style lock gate (one leaf, 3.6 wide, 1.2 tall, 0.5 thick) that sinks into a slot in the felt to open (offset -1.3: top 0.1 below the felt) and rises to close (offset 0). It is down while the platform waits at the bottom and up while the platform rides
- **lift lock_lift**: rectangle centre [0.0, 0.0, -23.25] +- [1.5, 1.65] -> exit [0.0, 4.0, -27.0] dir [0.0, -1.0], exit speed = clamp(in x 0.5, 1.5, 7.0) studs/s. Lock chamber floor = lift platform: a ball that rolls onto it (past the sunken gate) is lifted to the quay and pushed out by the sluice rush (exit speed scales with the entry speed) Ride: while the gate is up: rise 4.0 at 2.0 studs/s (2 s), tilt 4 deg north and release the ball onto the quay at (0, 4, -27) heading -Z at clamp(entry speed x 0.5, 1.5, 7) studs/s, then drop back (1.5 s) before the gate sinks again (platform: 3.2 x 4.0 lock-chamber floor (the felt piece 'lock') is the lift platform; lock cycle 9.6 s).

## Timing check (`tools/timing_check.py`, 24 phases, 100k-shot soft-lock fuzz)
- lock_gate [intended] tee shot into the lock chamber over the sunken gate: blocked 10/24 (10 stops + 0 kicks), deflected 0, **Studio estimate 41.7%** (stops + 0.36 kicks), passed 58.3%, traps 0, fuzz soft-locks 0/100000
- lock_gate [ace] brute-force ace line: blocked 10/24 (10 stops + 0 kicks), deflected 0, **Studio estimate 41.7%** (stops + 0.36 kicks), passed 58.3%, traps 0

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [0.0, 11.5, -43.0]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +3.92 studs).

# Hole 12: Intake Tower (Par 4)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Dam**. Gimmick: **Pipe teleport: sink the ball in the intake grate at the end of a rail-less pier and it shoots out of an outflow pipe by the green, or play the long shoreline route**.
Layout: `h12-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[11]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [-200.0, 116.0, -270.0], yaw -90.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [2.0, 0.0, -58.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
A domed peppermint **intake tower** stands in Sugarloaf Reservoir beside a rail-less timber pier with a glowing brass intake grate.

## Gimmick
Pipe teleport: sink the ball in the intake grate at the end of a rail-less pier and it shoots out of an outflow pipe by the green, or play the long shoreline route.

## Intended shots
**Shortcut:** 1) putt along the pier and sink the ball in the intake grate 2 studs from the open end (r 0.3); it shoots out of the outflow pipe on the green aimed at the cup. 2) putt.
**Shoreline route:** 1) into the shoreline channel, 2) up it to the green, 3-4) putt across.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (30 grid shots). The most robust: aim **269.5°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (-0.01, -1.00) in x/z, i.e. 0.5° left of straight -Z), speed **16.8 studs/s** (85% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Risk/reward: overshoot the pier and the ball goes in the reservoir (+1, back to the stroke start). The shoreline is the reliable par.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- **teleport intake_pipe**: entry [0.0, 0.0, -33.0] r 0.3 -> exit [11.0, 0.0, -61.0] dir [-0.9487, 0.3162], exit speed = clamp(in x 0.7, 3.0, 7.0) studs/s. Brass intake grate (glowing lime ring) set in the pier deck 2 studs from its open end -> outflow pipe mouth on the green's east side
- **hazard reservoir** (water): +1 stroke, ball back to where the stroke started

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [2.0, 7.5, -58.0]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +4.81 studs).

- `hour_stones` at [0.0, 0, -36.0] size [22, 1.2, 22]: 12 candy-coloured hour stones set in the rail ring (outside the rails), roman numerals



h18-notes.md
# Hole 18: Sunset Arch (Par 4)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Crown**. Gimmick: **Finale: bank round a hoodoo, ramp up under the arch, then a geode-shortcut teleport or the rim route to a cliff-edge green**.
Layout: `h18-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[17]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [-420.0, 190.0, 236.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [8.0, 0.8, -64.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
A natural candy-striped **arch** spans the ramp, framing the sunset; a twin **geode** shortcut glows on the plateau; the cliff-edge green looks over the whole canyon.

## Gimmick
Finale: bank round a hoodoo, ramp up under the arch, then a geode-shortcut teleport or the rim route to a cliff-edge green.

## Intended shots
1. Play round the hoodoo at the end of the tee lane into the banked NE corner, west past the junction into the banked NW corner and up the ramp under the arch.
2. From the plateau: into the geode (it pops out by the cup) for a birdie try, or up the rim lane.
3-4. Putt on the cliff-edge green.
Ace: a full tee shot that rides both banks and the ramp straight into the geode.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (5 grid shots). The most robust: aim **254.5°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (-0.27, -0.96) in x/z, i.e. 15.5° left of straight -Z), speed **18.7 studs/s** (94% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
The finale strings the course together: bank, climb, shortcut or safe route. The geode exit is soft (half the entry speed, from 1.5): only a ball arriving fast holes out, a gentle plateau putt leaves a tap-in.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- **teleport geode**: entry [-9.5, 0.8, -32.0] r 0.55 -> exit [13.0, 0.8, -59.0] dir [-0.7071, -0.7071], exit speed = clamp(in x 0.7, 1.5, 7.0) studs/s. Cracked-open amethyst-and-rock-candy geode set in the plateau felt straight up the ramp line (entry) -> twin geode on the cliff-edge green (exit), aimed at the cup

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [8.0, 8.3, -64.0]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +3.26 studs).


## Props for the 3D modeler (decoration only, sizes in studs)
Natural arch over the ramp (P1, landmark); hoodoo with gumdrop cap (P1); geode entry + exit (P1); cliff-edge rope railing (P1); rim sign (P1); felt arrow (P1); flag 7.5 (P1). The big Sunset Arch backdrop is world dressing.

Decoration anchors (position in hole-local studs):
- `cup_flag` at [8.0, 0.8, -64.0] size [2.0, 7.5, 0.15]: Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 dia) in the cup, 2.0x1.2 banner at the top, turns to face the tee; no collision
- `arch_gate` at [-9, 0, -26] (LANDMARK) size [10, 13, 4]: Natural candy-striped sandstone arch spanning the ramp (feet outside the rails at x -13.25 and -4.75, opening 7.5 wide x 10 high), framing the sunset to the west
- `hoodoo_deco` at [0.0, 0, -11.0] size [2.2, 10, 2.2]: Striped hoodoo with a gumdrop cap on the 1.6 bumper (turned 45) at the end of the tee lane: play round it into the banked corner
- `geode_entry` at [-9.5, 0.8, -32.0] size [1.6, 0.3, 1.6]: Cracked geode bowl flush in the plateau felt, glowing violet (trigger radius 0.55)
- `geode_exit` at [13.0, 0.8, -59.0] size [1.6, 0.6, 1.6]: Twin geode on the green's east side (teleport exit)
- `cliff_edge` at [0, 0.8, -70] size [26, 2, 1]: Cliff-edge rope railing on carved posts along the green's north and east rails; the Crown drops away to the canyon beyond (the sunset view)
- `sign_rim` at [-12.6, 0.8, -42] size [1.8, 3.2, 0.25]: Sign 'Rim Trail' pointing north along the rim
- `felt_arrow_bank` at [1.0, 0.0, -10.0] size [2.0, 0.01, 2.6]: Glowing felt arrow decal curving right-then-left: 'bank it round the hoodoo'

| H18 | Plateau shot power scale 1.3 -> **1.4** | Average ramp shots rolled back: caps 2.2% -> **0.3%** (ps 1.4 + rim carry 6: 0.2% but no better elsewhere) |
| H2 | Decoration only: rock face over the portals capped at y 5, headframe 1 stud east (occluders updated) | Flag sightline was blocked by those props (-9.6); now **visible, +1.2** |

# Hole 1: Trailhead Gate (Par 2)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Junction**. Gimmick: **Bank shot: a striped boulder's 45-degree face turns the ball east onto an offset green**.
Layout: `h01-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[0]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [60.0, 18.0, 470.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [6.0, 0.0, -30.5] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
A candy-striped timber **trailhead arch** spans the lane with a swirl-lollipop sign; at the far end a big pink-and-cream striped **boulder** sits in the back-left corner of the green, its flat south-east face turned 45° to the lane.

## Gimmick
Bank shot: a striped boulder's 45-degree face turns the ball east onto an offset green.

## Intended shots
1. Roll straight up the 7-wide lane into the boulder's 45° face: it turns the ball east along the back of the offset green.
2. The cup sits off that line toward the front of the green, so the second putt comes back toward you (two-putt par).

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (35 grid shots). The most robust: aim **249.5°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (-0.35, -0.94) in x/z, i.e. 20.5° left of straight -Z), speed **19.0 studs/s** (96% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
Teaches the bank. Pace matters more than line: too soft and the ball dies on the face, too firm and it runs the length of the green into the east rail. The ace needs a slightly angled tee shot so the bank sends the ball back toward the cup.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- none

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [6.0, 7.5, -30.5]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +5.50 studs).

# Hole 18: Sunset Arch (Par 4)

Zone: **Sugarloaf Crown**. Gimmick: **Finale: bank round a hoodoo, ramp up under the arch, then a geode-shortcut teleport or the rim route to a cliff-edge green**.
Layout: `h18-layout.png`. Geometry: `holes.json` -> holes[17]. World placement (from `world/world.json`): origin [-420.0, 190.0, 236.0], yaw 0.0.
Tee [0, 0, 0], cup [8.0, 0.8, -64.0] (radius 0.5). Units: studs (1 stud = 0.3 m).

## Landmark
A natural candy-striped **arch** spans the ramp, framing the sunset; a twin **geode** shortcut glows on the plateau; the cliff-edge green looks over the whole canyon.

## Gimmick
Finale: bank round a hoodoo, ramp up under the arch, then a geode-shortcut teleport or the rim route to a cliff-edge green.

## Intended shots
1. Play round the hoodoo at the end of the tee lane into the banked NE corner, west past the junction into the banked NW corner and up the ramp under the arch.
2. From the plateau: into the geode (it pops out by the cup) for a birdie try, or up the rim lane.
3-4. Putt on the cliff-edge green.
Ace: a full tee shot that rides both banks and the ramp straight into the geode.

## Hole-in-one line
Brute-force search from the tee found ace lines (5 grid shots). The most robust: aim **254.5°** (atan2(dz,dx): direction (-0.27, -0.96) in x/z, i.e. 15.5° left of straight -Z), speed **18.7 studs/s** (94% power). The red dashed line on the layout PNG traces it.

## Challenge
The finale strings the course together: bank, climb, shortcut or safe route. The geode exit is soft (half the entry speed, from 1.5): only a ball arriving fast holes out, a gentle plateau putt leaves a tap-in.

## Moving parts / teleports / hazards (exact specs, also in holes.json)
- **teleport geode**: entry [-9.5, 0.8, -32.0] r 0.55 -> exit [13.0, 0.8, -59.0] dir [-0.7071, -0.7071], exit speed = clamp(in x 0.7, 1.5, 7.0) studs/s. Cracked-open amethyst-and-rock-candy geode set in the plateau felt straight up the ramp line (entry) -> twin geode on the cliff-edge green (exit), aimed at the cup

## Flag and sightline
Flag: 7.5-stud pole (0.15 diameter) in the cup, [2.0, 1.2, 0.05] #e03c31 banner at the top [8.0, 8.3, -64.0]; visual only, no collision. Tee eye (5.5 up) -> flag top: **visible** (min clearance +3.26 studs).
